"""Entrada para ejecutar la interfaz Flet sola (`flet run` o `flet build`).

Uso (desde la raíz del proyecto):

    uv run flet run

Requiere el backend en marcha en http://127.0.0.1:8000 (ver frontend/run.py).
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Permite importar frontend.* al ejecutar el script directamente
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.main_view import main

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ft.run(main)


======================================================================
--- RUTA: run.py ---
======================================================================

"""Servidor de desarrollo: API REST + interfaz Flet en un único proceso.

Uso (desde la raíz del proyecto):

    uv run python frontend/run.py

La API queda en http://127.0.0.1:8000 (Swagger en /docs) y la interfaz Flet
se sirve en la raíz http://127.0.0.1:8000/.
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Permite importar backend.* y frontend.* al ejecutar el script directamente
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))

import uvicorn

if __name__ == "__main__":
    uvicorn.run("backend.app.main:app", host="0.0.0.0", port=8000, reload=True)


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\api_client.py ---
======================================================================

import os
from typing import Any, Optional

import httpx


class APIClient:
    """Cliente HTTP asíncrono centralizado para interactuar con la API REST de FastAPI.

    La URL base se puede configurar con la variable de entorno API_BASE_URL
    (por defecto http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1) para poder desplegar el sistema
    en otro servidor sin tocar el código.
    """

    _instance: Optional["APIClient"] = None

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if cls._instance is None:
            cls._instance = super().__new__(cls)
            cls._instance._initialized = False
        return cls._instance

    def __init__(self, base_url: str | None = None):
        if getattr(self, "_initialized", False):
            return

        self.base_url = (
            base_url or os.getenv("API_BASE_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1")
        ).rstrip("/")
        self.token: str | None = None
        self._client: httpx.AsyncClient | None = None
        self._initialized = True

    def _get_client(self) -> httpx.AsyncClient:
        if self._client is None or self._client.is_closed:
            self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=10.0)
        return self._client

    def set_token(self, token: str) -> None:
        self.token = token

    def clear_token(self) -> None:
        self.token = None

    def _get_headers(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
        if self.token:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self.token}"
        return headers

    # ==================== HELPERS INTERNOS ====================
    async def _get(self, path: str, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> Any:
        client = self._get_client()
        response = await client.get(
            f"{self.base_url}{path}", params=params, headers=self._get_headers()
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()

    async def _post(self, path: str, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        client = self._get_client()
        response = await client.post(
            f"{self.base_url}{path}", json=data, headers=self._get_headers()
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()

    async def _put(self, path: str, data: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        client = self._get_client()
        response = await client.put(
            f"{self.base_url}{path}", json=data, headers=self._get_headers()
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()

    async def _delete(self, path: str) -> Any:
        client = self._get_client()
        response = await client.delete(
            f"{self.base_url}{path}", headers=self._get_headers()
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
        if response.content:
            return response.json()
        return None

    # ==================== AUTENTICACIÓN ====================
    async def login(self, username: str, password: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        client = self._get_client()
        url = f"{self.base_url}/auth/login"
        payload = {"username": username, "password": password}

        response = await client.post(
            url, json=payload, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}
        )
        response.raise_for_status()

        data = response.json()
        if "access_token" in data:
            self.set_token(data["access_token"])
        return data

    async def get_mi_perfil(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._get("/auth/me")

    async def cambiar_password(
        self, password_actual: str, password_nueva: str
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(
            "/auth/me/password",
            {"password_actual": password_actual, "password_nueva": password_nueva},
        )

    # ==================== USUARIOS Y ROLES ====================
    async def get_usuarios(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/auth/usuarios")

    async def get_roles(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/auth/roles")

    async def create_usuario(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/auth/usuarios", data)

    async def update_usuario(
        self, id_usuario: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/auth/usuarios/{id_usuario}", data)

    # ==================== ALUMNOS ====================
    async def get_alumnos(self, solo_activos: bool = True) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        client = self._get_client()
        url = f"{self.base_url}/personas/alumnos"
        params = {"solo_activos": str(solo_activos).lower(), "limit": 100}

        response = await client.get(url, params=params, headers=self._get_headers())
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()

    async def get_alumno(self, legajo: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._get(f"/personas/alumnos/{legajo}")

    async def create_alumno(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/personas/alumnos", data)

    async def update_alumno(self, legajo: int, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/personas/alumnos/{legajo}", data)

    async def delete_alumno(self, legajo: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/personas/alumnos/{legajo}")

    # ==================== PROFESORES ====================
    async def get_profesores(self, solo_activos: bool = True) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        client = self._get_client()
        url = f"{self.base_url}/personas/profesores"
        params = {"solo_activos": str(solo_activos).lower(), "limit": 100}

        response = await client.get(url, params=params, headers=self._get_headers())
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()

    async def create_profesor(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/personas/profesores", data)

    async def update_profesor(
        self, legajo: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/personas/profesores/{legajo}", data)

    async def delete_profesor(self, legajo: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/personas/profesores/{legajo}")

    # ==================== PADRES / TUTORES ====================
    async def get_padres(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/personas/padres")

    async def get_hijos(self, id_padre: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get(f"/personas/padres/{id_padre}/alumnos")

    async def create_padre(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/personas/padres", data)

    # ==================== NIVELES EDUCATIVOS ====================
    async def get_niveles(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/academico/niveles")

    async def create_nivel(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/academico/niveles", data)

    async def update_nivel(self, id_nivel: int, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/academico/niveles/{id_nivel}", data)

    async def delete_nivel(self, id_nivel: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/academico/niveles/{id_nivel}")

    # ==================== CURSOS ====================
    async def get_cursos(self, id_nivel: int | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        params = {"id_nivel": id_nivel} if id_nivel is not None else None
        return await self._get("/academico/cursos", params=params)

    async def create_curso(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/academico/cursos", data)

    async def update_curso(self, id_curso: int, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/academico/cursos/{id_curso}", data)

    async def delete_curso(self, id_curso: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/academico/cursos/{id_curso}")

    # ==================== MATERIAS ====================
    async def get_materias(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/academico/materias")

    async def create_materia(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/academico/materias", data)

    async def update_materia(
        self, id_materia: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/academico/materias/{id_materia}", data)

    async def delete_materia(self, id_materia: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/academico/materias/{id_materia}")

    # ==================== ASIGNACIONES (materia-curso-profesor) ====================
    async def get_asignaciones(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/academico/asignaciones")

    async def create_asignacion(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/academico/asignaciones", data)

    async def update_asignacion(
        self, id_materia: int, id_curso: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/academico/asignaciones/{id_materia}/{id_curso}", data)

    async def delete_asignacion(self, id_materia: int, id_curso: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/academico/asignaciones/{id_materia}/{id_curso}")

    # ==================== HORARIOS ====================
    async def get_horarios(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/academico/horarios")

    async def create_horario(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/academico/horarios", data)

    async def update_horario(
        self, id_horario: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/academico/horarios/{id_horario}", data)

    async def delete_horario(self, id_horario: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/academico/horarios/{id_horario}")

    # ==================== CALIFICACIONES (notas) ====================
    async def get_calificaciones(
        self,
        legajo_alumno: int | None = None,
        id_materia: int | None = None,
        id_curso: int | None = None,
        periodo: str | None = None,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        params: dict[str, Any] = {}
        if legajo_alumno is not None:
            params["legajo_alumno"] = legajo_alumno
        if id_materia is not None:
            params["id_materia"] = id_materia
        if id_curso is not None:
            params["id_curso"] = id_curso
        if periodo:
            params["periodo"] = periodo
        return await self._get("/academico/calificaciones", params=params or None)

    async def crear_calificacion(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/academico/calificaciones", data)

    async def actualizar_calificacion(
        self, id_calificacion: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/academico/calificaciones/{id_calificacion}", data)

    async def eliminar_calificacion(self, id_calificacion: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/academico/calificaciones/{id_calificacion}")

    # ==================== ASISTENCIA ====================
    async def get_asistencia(
        self,
        id_curso: int | None = None,
        fecha: str | None = None,
        legajo_alumno: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        params: dict[str, Any] = {}
        if id_curso is not None:
            params["id_curso"] = id_curso
        if fecha:
            params["fecha"] = fecha
        if legajo_alumno is not None:
            params["legajo_alumno"] = legajo_alumno
        return await self._get("/academico/asistencia", params=params or None)

    async def registrar_asistencia_lote(
        self, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._post("/academico/asistencia/lote", data)

    async def get_resumen_asistencia(self, legajo_alumno: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._get(f"/academico/asistencia/resumen/{legajo_alumno}")

    async def eliminar_asistencia(self, id_asistencia: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/academico/asistencia/{id_asistencia}")

    # ==================== DEPORTES ====================
    async def get_deportes(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/servicios/deportes")

    async def create_deporte(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/servicios/deportes", data)

    async def update_deporte(
        self, id_deporte: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/servicios/deportes/{id_deporte}", data)

    async def delete_deporte(self, id_deporte: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/servicios/deportes/{id_deporte}")

    async def get_grupos_deporte(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/servicios/deportes/grupos")

    async def create_grupo_deporte(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/servicios/deportes/grupos", data)

    async def update_grupo_deporte(
        self, id_grupo: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/servicios/deportes/grupos/{id_grupo}", data)

    async def delete_grupo_deporte(self, id_grupo: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/servicios/deportes/grupos/{id_grupo}")

    async def get_inscripciones_deporte(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/servicios/deportes/inscripciones")

    async def inscribir_deporte(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/servicios/deportes/inscripciones", data)

    # ==================== TRANSPORTE ====================
    async def get_recorridos(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/servicios/transporte/recorridos")

    async def create_recorrido(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/servicios/transporte/recorridos", data)

    async def update_recorrido(
        self, id_recorrido: int, data: dict[str, Any]
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/servicios/transporte/recorridos/{id_recorrido}", data)

    async def delete_recorrido(self, id_recorrido: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/servicios/transporte/recorridos/{id_recorrido}")

    async def get_inscripciones_transporte(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/servicios/transporte/inscripciones")

    async def inscribir_transporte(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/servicios/transporte/inscripciones", data)

    async def baja_transporte(self, legajo_alumno: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(
            f"/servicios/transporte/inscripciones/{legajo_alumno}"
        )

    # ==================== COMEDOR ====================
    async def get_inscripciones_comedor(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/servicios/comedor/inscripciones")

    async def inscribir_comedor(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/servicios/comedor/inscripciones", data)

    async def baja_comedor(self, legajo_alumno: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/servicios/comedor/inscripciones/{legajo_alumno}")

    # ==================== COMUNIDAD (admisiones, contacto, opiniones, empleo) ====================
    async def crear_solicitud(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/comunidad/solicitudes", data)

    async def get_solicitudes(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/comunidad/solicitudes")

    async def cambiar_estado_solicitud(
        self, id_solicitud: int, estado: str, observaciones: str | None = None
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {"estado": estado}
        if observaciones is not None:
            payload["observaciones"] = observaciones
        return await self._put(f"/comunidad/solicitudes/{id_solicitud}/estado", payload)

    async def enviar_contacto(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/comunidad/contacto", data)

    async def get_mensajes_contacto(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/comunidad/contacto")

    async def marcar_mensaje_leido(self, id_mensaje: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/comunidad/contacto/{id_mensaje}/leido", {})

    async def crear_opinion(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/comunidad/opiniones", data)

    async def get_opiniones(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/comunidad/opiniones")

    async def get_opiniones_todas(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/comunidad/opiniones/todas")

    async def aprobar_opinion(self, id_opinion: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._put(f"/comunidad/opiniones/{id_opinion}/aprobar", {})

    async def eliminar_opinion(self, id_opinion: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._delete(f"/comunidad/opiniones/{id_opinion}")

    async def enviar_postulacion(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._post("/comunidad/postulaciones", data)

    async def get_postulaciones(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return await self._get("/comunidad/postulaciones")

    # ==================== DASHBOARD ====================
    async def get_resumen_dashboard(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._get("/dashboard/resumen")

    # ==================== REPORTES EXCEL ====================
    def reporte_url(self, nombre: str) -> str:
        """URL absoluta de descarga de un reporte Excel, con el token de sesión."""
        return f"{self.base_url}/reportes/{nombre}.xlsx?token={self.token or ''}"


api_client = APIClient()

SECCION_POR_ROL = {
    "ADMIN": "admin",
    "TEACHER": "docente",
    "DOCENTE": "docente",
    "PARENT": "padre",
    "FAMILIA": "padre",
    "STUDENT": "alumno",
    "ALUMNO": "alumno",
}

    "docente": "/portal/docente/dashboard",

    "alumno": "/portal/alumno/dashboard",
}


async def main(page: ft.Page):
    page.title = "Educar para Transformar"
    page.padding = 0

    async def route_change(e: ft.RouteChangeEvent):
        page.views.clear()
        ruta = (page.route or "/").rstrip("/")
        if not ruta:
            ruta = "/"

        # ==================== 1. GUARDIAS DE SESIÓN Y ROL ====================
        if ruta.startswith("/admin") or ruta.startswith("/portal/"):
            token = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
            if not token:
                # Sin sesión activa: se muestra el login
                ruta = "/portal"
            else:
                rol = (getattr(page, "auth_rol", "") or "").upper()
                seccion_permitida = SECCION_POR_ROL.get(rol)
                if ruta.startswith("/admin"):
                    seccion_requerida = "admin"
                elif "/docente/" in ruta:
                    seccion_requerida = "docente"
                elif "/padre/" in ruta:
                    seccion_requerida = "padre"
                else:
                    seccion_requerida = "alumno"
                if seccion_permitida != seccion_requerida:
                    # El rol no corresponde a esta sección: se deriva a su panel
                    ruta = RUTA_INICIAL_POR_ROL.get(seccion_permitida or "", "/portal")

        # ==================== 2. RUTAS ADMINISTRATIVAS ====================
        if ruta == "/admin/dashboard":
            vista_dash = await get_admin_dashboard_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_dash]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/alumnos":
            vista_alumnos = await get_admin_alumnos_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_alumnos]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/alumnos/nuevo":
            vista_form = await get_admin_alumno_form_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_form]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/profesores":
            vista_profesores = await get_admin_profesores_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_profesores])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/profesores/nuevo":
            vista_profesor_form = await get_admin_profesor_form_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_profesor_form])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/reportes":
            vista_reportes = await get_admin_reportes_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_reportes]))
            page.update()

        elif ruta == "/admin/deportes":

            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/transporte":
            vista_transporte = await get_admin_transporte_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_transporte])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/comedor":
            vista_comedor = await get_admin_comedor_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_comedor]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/niveles":
            vista_niveles = await get_admin_niveles_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_niveles]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/cursos":
            vista_cursos = await get_admin_cursos_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_cursos]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/materias":
            vista_materias = await get_admin_materias_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_materias]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/horarios":
            vista_horarios = await get_admin_horarios_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_horarios]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/inscripciones":
            vista_inscripciones = await get_admin_inscripciones_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_inscripciones])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/comunidad":
            vista_comunidad = await get_admin_comunidad_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_comunidad])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/usuarios":
            vista_usuarios = await get_admin_usuarios_view(page)
            page.views.append(ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_usuarios]))
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/admin/reporte-alumnos-docentes":
            vista_reporte_ad = await get_admin_reporte_alumnos_docentes_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_reporte_ad])

            page.update()

        elif ruta == "/admin/reporte-cursos-materias":
            vista_reporte_cm = await get_admin_reporte_cursos_materias_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_reporte_cm])

            page.update()

        elif ruta == "/admin/reporte-deportes-avanzado":
            vista_reporte_dep = await get_admin_reporte_deportes_avanzado_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_reporte_dep])

        elif ruta == "/admin/reporte-transporte-pasajeros":

        # ==================== 3. RUTAS DEL PORTAL FAMILIAS ====================

            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_padre_notas])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        # ==================== 4. RUTAS DEL PORTAL DOCENTE ====================
        if ruta == "/portal/docente/dashboard":
            vista_docente_dash = await get_docente_dashboard_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_docente_dash])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/docente/materias":
            vista_docente_mat = await get_docente_materias_view(page)
            page.views.append(

            )

            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/docente/alumnos":
            vista_docente_alu = await get_docente_alumnos_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_docente_alu])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/docente/deportes":
            vista_docente_dep = await get_docente_deportes_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_docente_dep])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/docente/notas":
            vista_docente_notas = await get_docente_notas_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_docente_notas])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        # ==================== 5. RUTAS DEL PORTAL ESTUDIANTE ====================
        if ruta == "/portal/alumno/dashboard":
            vista_alumno_dash = await get_alumno_dashboard_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_alumno_dash])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/alumno/materias":
            vista_alumno_mat = await get_alumno_materias_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_alumno_mat])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/alumno/deportes":
            vista_alumno_dep = await get_alumno_deportes_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_alumno_dep])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/alumno/servicios":
            vista_alumno_serv = await get_alumno_servicios_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_alumno_serv])
            )
            page.update()
            return
        elif ruta == "/portal/alumno/notas":
            vista_alumno_notas = await get_alumno_notas_view(page)
            page.views.append(
                ft.View(route=ruta, padding=0, controls=[vista_alumno_notas])
            )
            page.update()
            return

        # ==================== 6. RUTAS PÚBLICAS INSTITUCIONALES ====================
        if ruta == "/quienes-somos":
            contenido_central = get_quienes_somos_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/inscripcion":
            contenido_central = get_inscripcion_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/noticias":
            contenido_central = get_noticias_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/niveles":
            contenido_central = get_niveles_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/bienestar-estudiantil":
            contenido_central = get_bienestar_estudiantil_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/opiniones":
            contenido_central = await get_opiniones_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/empleo":
            contenido_central = get_empleo_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/contacto":
            contenido_central = get_contacto_view(page)
        elif ruta == "/portal":
            contenido_central = get_login_view(page)
        else:
            contenido_central = home_view(page)

        page.views.append(
            ft.View(
                route=ruta,
                padding=0,
                scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
                controls=[
                    create_navbar(page, active_route=ruta),
                    *contenido_central,
                    create_footer(page),
                ],
            )
        )
        page.update()

    def view_pop(e: ft.ViewPopEvent):
        page.views.pop()
        top_view = page.views[-1]
        page.route = top_view.route
        page.update()

    page.on_route_change = route_change
    page.on_view_pop = view_pop

    await route_change(None)

======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\components\admin_layout.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.components.constants import BG_PAGE
from frontend.app.components.sidebar import create_sidebar
from frontend.app.components.topbar import create_topbar


def admin_layout(
    page: ft.Page,
    titulo: str,
    active_route: str,
    content_controls: list[ft.Control],
    scroll_mode: ft.ScrollMode = ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
    padding: int = 32,
    spacing: int = 24,
) -> ft.Row:
    """Layout maestro administrativo con sidebar colapsable reactivo."""

    # Estado de colapso guardado directamente en la instancia de la página del usuario
    is_collapsed = getattr(page, "sidebar_collapsed", False)

    sidebar_holder = ft.Container()
    topbar = create_topbar(page, titulo=titulo)

    def toggle_sidebar(e):
        nuevo_estado = not getattr(page, "sidebar_collapsed", False)
        page.sidebar_collapsed = nuevo_estado
        sidebar_holder.content = create_sidebar(
            page,
            active_route=active_route,
            collapsed=nuevo_estado,
            on_toggle=toggle_sidebar,
        )
        sidebar_holder.update()

    sidebar_holder.content = create_sidebar(
        page,
        active_route=active_route,
        collapsed=is_collapsed,
        on_toggle=toggle_sidebar,
    )

    main_content = ft.Container(
        expand=True,
        bgcolor=BG_PAGE,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=0,
            controls=[
                topbar,
                ft.Container(
                    expand=True,
                    padding=padding,
                    content=ft.Column(
                        spacing=spacing,
                        scroll=scroll_mode,
                        controls=content_controls,
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    return ft.Row(
        expand=True,
        spacing=0,
        controls=[sidebar_holder, main_content],
    )

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_GREEN_BG,
    BADGE_GREEN_TXT,
    BADGE_RED_BG,
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BADGE_YELLOW_BG,
    BADGE_YELLOW_TXT,
    BG_PAGE,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)


def input_con_label(
    label: str,
    hint: str = "",
    width: int | None = 289,
    expand: bool = False,
    read_only: bool = False,
    valor_inicial: str | None = None,
    keyboard_type: ft.KeyboardType = ft.KeyboardType.TEXT,
    password: bool = False,
) -> ft.Column:
    """Genera un input estandarizado con su etiqueta superior."""
    text_field = ft.TextField(
        value=valor_inicial or "",
        hint_text=hint if not valor_inicial else None,
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=MUTED if read_only else TEXT_COLOR,
        read_only=read_only,
        password=password,
        keyboard_type=keyboard_type,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )

    return ft.Column(
        spacing=6,
        width=width,
        expand=expand,
        controls=[
            ft.Text(
                label,
                size=13,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                color=MUTED if read_only else TEXT_COLOR,
            ),
            ft.Container(
                height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
                bgcolor=BG_PAGE if read_only else BG_WHITE,
                border=None if read_only else ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
                alignment=ft.Alignment(-1, 0),
                content=text_field,
            ),
        ],
    )


def dropdown_con_label(
    label: str,
    valor_default: str | None,
    opciones: list[str],
    width: int | None = 187,
    expand: bool = False,
) -> ft.Column:
    """Genera un selector desplegable estandarizado con su etiqueta superior."""
    return ft.Column(
        spacing=6,
        width=width,
        expand=expand,
        controls=[
            ft.Text(label, size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=TEXT_COLOR),
            ft.Container(
                height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
                bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                content=ft.Dropdown(
                    value=valor_default,
                    text_size=14,
                    border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
                    content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
                    options=[ft.dropdown.Option(opc) for opc in opciones],
                ),
            ),
        ],
    )


def status_badge(estado: str) -> ft.Container:
    """Genera un badge de estado con colores semánticos consistentes."""
    estado_normalizado = (estado or "").strip().lower()

    if estado_normalizado in ["activo", "regular", "aprobado", "al día"]:
        bg, color = BADGE_GREEN_BG, BADGE_GREEN_TXT
    elif estado_normalizado in ["pendiente", "licencia", "en revisión"]:
        bg, color = BADGE_YELLOW_BG, BADGE_YELLOW_TXT
    else:
        bg, color = BADGE_RED_BG, BADGE_RED_TXT

    return ft.Container(
        padding=ft.Padding(8, 4, 8, 4),
        bgcolor=bg,
        border_radius=4,
        content=ft.Text(estado, size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=color),
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\components\navbar.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)


def create_navbar(page: ft.Page, active_route: str = "/") -> ft.Container:
    def navegar(ruta: str):
        page.run_task(page.push_route, ruta)

    def nav_button(texto: str, ruta: str):
        es_activo = active_route == ruta
        return ft.Button(
            content=ft.Text(texto),
            bgcolor="transparent",
            color=TEAL if es_activo else TEXT_COLOR,
            elevation=0,
            style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                overlay_color="transparent",
                elevation=0,
                shadow_color="transparent",
            ),
            on_click=lambda _: navegar(ruta),
        )

    return ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        height=90,
        padding=ft.Padding.symmetric(horizontal=40),
        border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
        content=ft.Row(
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.GestureDetector(
                    mouse_cursor=ft.MouseCursor.CLICK,
                    on_tap=lambda _: navegar("/"),
                    content=ft.Row(
                        spacing=12,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Container(
                                width=40,
                                height=40,
                                bgcolor=TEAL,
                                border_radius=20,
                                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                content=ft.Icon(
                                    ft.Icons.SCHOOL, color="white", size=22
                                ),
                            ),
                            ft.Column(
                                spacing=0,
                                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                                controls=[
                                    ft.Text(
                                        "Educar para Transformar",
                                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                        size=18,
                                        color=TEAL_DARK,
                                    ),
                                    ft.Text("RESISTENCIA · K-12", size=11, color=MUTED),
                                ],
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Row(
                    spacing=4,
                    scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
                    controls=[
                        nav_button("Inicio", "/"),
                        nav_button("Quiénes Somos", "/quienes-somos"),
                        nav_button("Niveles", "/niveles"),
                        nav_button("Bienestar", "/bienestar-estudiantil"),
                        nav_button("Inscripción", "/inscripcion"),
                        nav_button("Noticias", "/noticias"),
                        nav_button("Opiniones", "/opiniones"),
                        nav_button("Contacto", "/contacto"),
                        ft.Button(
                            "PORTAL ACCESO",
                            bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                            elevation=0,
                            style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                                shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=8)
                            ),
                            on_click=lambda _: navegar("/portal"),
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\components\password_dialog.py ---
======================================================================

"""Diálogo reutilizable para que el usuario autenticado cambie su contraseña."""

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
)


def abrir_cambio_password(page: ft.Page):
    """Abre el diálogo modal de cambio de contraseña del usuario autenticado."""

    actual = ft.TextField(
        label="Contraseña actual",
        password=True,
        can_reveal_password=True,
        width=340,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
    )
    nueva = ft.TextField(
        label="Nueva contraseña (mínimo 6 caracteres)",
        password=True,
        can_reveal_password=True,
        width=340,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
    )
    repetir = ft.TextField(
        label="Repetir nueva contraseña",
        password=True,
        can_reveal_password=True,
        width=340,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
    )
    mensaje = ft.Text("", size=12, color="#EF4444")

    async def guardar(e):
        mensaje.value = ""
        if not actual.value or not nueva.value or not repetir.value:
            mensaje.value = "Complete todos los campos para continuar."
            page.update()
            return
        if nueva.value != repetir.value:
            mensaje.value = "Las contraseñas nuevas no coinciden."
            page.update()
            return

        try:
            await api_client.cambiar_password(actual.value, nueva.value)
        except httpx.HTTPStatusError as error:
            detalle = "No se pudo cambiar la contraseña."
            try:
                detalle = error.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
            except Exception:
                pass
            mensaje.value = detalle
            page.update()
            return
        except Exception:
            mensaje.value = "No se pudo conectar con el servidor."
            page.update()
            return

        page.pop_dialog()
        aviso = ft.SnackBar(
            ft.Text("Contraseña actualizada correctamente.", color="white"),
            bgcolor=TEAL,
        )
        page.show_dialog(aviso)

    dialog = ft.AlertDialog(
        modal=True,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        title=ft.Text(
            "Cambiar mi contraseña", size=16, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEAL_DARK
        ),
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=14,
            width=360,
            tight=True,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(
                    "La nueva contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres.",
                    size=12,
                    color=MUTED,
                ),
                actual,
                nueva,
                repetir,
                mensaje,
            ],
        ),
        actions=[
            ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda _: page.pop_dialog()),
            ft.Button("Guardar", bgcolor=TEAL, color="white", on_click=guardar),
        ],
        actions_alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
    )
    page.show_dialog(dialog)


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\components\portal_layout.py ---

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BG_PAGE,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.password_dialog import abrir_cambio_password


def portal_layout(
    page: ft.Page,
    titulo: str,
    active_route: str,
    content_controls: list[ft.Control],
    rol: str = "Padre",
    usuario_nombre: str = "Martín Gómez",
    usuario_sub: str = "Tutor responsable",
    spacing: int = 24,
) -> ft.Row:
    """
    Layout maestro para los portales de autogestión (Padres, Docentes y Alumnos).
    Adapta el sidebar y topbar con accesos pertinentes al rol autenticado.
    """

    def _cerrar_sesion(_):
        """Cierra la sesión del usuario: borra el token y vuelve al login."""
        page.auth_token = None
        page.auth_rol = None
        page.auth_user = None
        api_client.clear_token()
        page.go("/portal")

    # Nombre y rol reales del usuario logueado (si están disponibles en la página)
    auth_user = getattr(page, "auth_user", None)
    if auth_user:
        usuario_nombre = auth_user
    rol_legible = {
        "ADMIN": "Administración",
        "TEACHER": "Docente",
        "DOCENTE": "Docente",
        "PARENT": "Tutor responsable",
        "FAMILIA": "Tutor responsable",
        "STUDENT": "Estudiante",
        "ALUMNO": "Estudiante",
    }.get((getattr(page, "auth_rol", "") or "").upper())
    if rol_legible:
        usuario_sub = rol_legible

    # 1. MENÚS SEGÚN ROL
    if rol.lower() in ["padre", "familia", "tutor"]:
        items_menu = [
            ("Mis Hijos", "/portal/padre/dashboard", ft.Icons.FAMILY_RESTROOM_OUTLINED),
            ("Notas y Asistencia", "/portal/padre/notas", ft.Icons.ASSESSMENT_OUTLINED),
            (
                "Inscripción Cursado",
                "/portal/padre/inscripcion",
                ft.Icons.APP_REGISTRATION_OUTLINED,
            ),
            (
                "Deportes y Horarios",
                "/portal/padre/deportes",
                ft.Icons.FITNESS_CENTER_OUTLINED,
            ),
            (
                "Comedor y Transporte",
                "/portal/padre/servicios",
                ft.Icons.RESTAURANT_OUTLINED,
            ),
            (
                "Docentes de mis Hijos",
                "/portal/padre/docentes",
                ft.Icons.SCHOOL_OUTLINED,
            ),
        ]
        portal_badge = "PORTAL FAMILIAS"
    elif rol.lower() in ["docente", "profesor"]:
        items_menu = [
            ("Mi Panel", "/portal/docente/dashboard", ft.Icons.DASHBOARD_OUTLINED),
            (
                "Mis Cursos y Materias",
                "/portal/docente/materias",
                ft.Icons.MENU_BOOK_OUTLINED,
            ),
            (
                "Notas y Asistencia",
                "/portal/docente/notas",
                ft.Icons.ASSESSMENT_OUTLINED,
            ),
            ("Nómina de Alumnos", "/portal/docente/alumnos", ft.Icons.PEOPLE_OUTLINE),
            (
                "Deportes a Cargo",
                "/portal/docente/deportes",
                ft.Icons.FITNESS_CENTER_OUTLINED,
            ),
        ]
        portal_badge = "PORTAL DOCENTE"
    else:  # Alumno
        items_menu = [
            ("Mi Cursado", "/portal/alumno/dashboard", ft.Icons.SCHOOL_OUTLINED),
            (
                "Materias y Profesores",
                "/portal/alumno/materias",
                ft.Icons.MENU_BOOK_OUTLINED,
            ),
            ("Mis Notas", "/portal/alumno/notas", ft.Icons.ASSESSMENT_OUTLINED),
            (
                "Mis Deportes",
                "/portal/alumno/deportes",
                ft.Icons.FITNESS_CENTER_OUTLINED,
            ),
            (
                "Comedor y Transporte",
                "/portal/alumno/servicios",
                ft.Icons.DIRECTIONS_BUS_OUTLINED,
            ),
        ]
        portal_badge = "PORTAL ESTUDIANTE"

    def item_sidebar(texto: str, ruta: str, icono: ft.IconData) -> ft.Container:
        es_activo = active_route == ruta
        return ft.Container(
            height=42,
            border_radius=8,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 12, 16, 12),
            bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT if es_activo else "transparent",
            on_click=lambda _: page.go(ruta),
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Icon(icono, size=18, color=TEAL_DARK if es_activo else MUTED),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            texto,
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600
                            if es_activo
                            else ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=TEAL_DARK if es_activo else TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=4,
                        height=16,
                        border_radius=2,
                        bgcolor=TEAL if es_activo else "transparent",
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    # 2. BRANDING DEL SIDEBAR
    brand_header = ft.Row(
        spacing=12,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Container(
                width=36,
                height=36,
                bgcolor=TEAL,
                border_radius=18,
                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SCHOOL, color="white", size=18),
            ),
            ft.Column(
                spacing=2,
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "Educar para Transformar",
                        size=14,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                        color=TEAL_DARK,
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        portal_badge, size=10, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=MUTED
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        ],
    )

    sidebar_container = ft.Container(
        width=260,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border(right=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
        padding=ft.Padding(16, 24, 16, 24),
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=18,
            controls=[
                brand_header,
                ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1, thickness=1),
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=4,
                    expand=True,
                    scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
                    controls=[item_sidebar(t, r, i) for t, r, i in items_menu],
                ),
                ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1, thickness=1),
                ft.Container(
                    height=40,
                    border_radius=8,
                    padding=ft.Padding(12, 8, 12, 8),
                    on_click=lambda _: abrir_cambio_password(page),
                    content=ft.Row(
                        spacing=10,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Icon(ft.Icons.LOCK_RESET, size=16, color=TEAL),
                            ft.Text(
                                "Cambiar Contraseña",
                                size=13,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    height=40,
                    border_radius=8,
                    padding=ft.Padding(12, 8, 12, 8),
                    on_click=_cerrar_sesion,
                    content=ft.Row(
                        spacing=10,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Icon(ft.Icons.LOGOUT, size=16, color="#EF4444"),
                            ft.Text(
                                "Cerrar Sesión",
                                size=13,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                color="#EF4444",
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    # 3. TOPBAR DEL PORTAL
    topbar_container = ft.Container(
        height=70,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
        padding=ft.Padding.symmetric(horizontal=32),
        content=ft.Row(
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(titulo, size=20, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEAL_DARK),
                ft.Row(
                    spacing=16,
                    vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Container(
                            width=36,
                            height=36,
                            bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                            border_radius=18,
                            alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                            content=ft.Icon(
                                ft.Icons.NOTIFICATIONS_NONE_OUTLINED,
                                size=18,
                                color=TEAL,
                            ),
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=10,
                            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Container(
                                    width=36,
                                    height=36,
                                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                                    border_radius=18,
                                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                    content=ft.Text(
                                        usuario_nombre[:2].upper(),
                                        size=13,
                                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                        color="white",
                                    ),
                                ),
                                ft.Column(
                                    spacing=1,
                                    alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                                    controls=[
                                        ft.Text(
                                            usuario_nombre,
                                            size=13,
                                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                                        ),
                                        ft.Text(usuario_sub, size=11, color=MUTED),
                                    ],
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    # 4. ÁREA DE CONTENIDO
    main_content = ft.Container(
        expand=True,
        bgcolor=BG_PAGE,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=0,
            controls=[
                topbar_container,
                ft.Container(
                    expand=True,
                    padding=32,
                    content=ft.Column(
                        spacing=spacing,
                        scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
                        controls=content_controls,
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    return ft.Row(
        expand=True,
        spacing=0,
        controls=[sidebar_container, main_content],
    )

--- RUTA: app\components\sidebar.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    MUTED,
    ONLINE_GREEN,
    SIDEBAR_WIDTH,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.password_dialog import abrir_cambio_password


def create_sidebar(
    page: ft.Page,
    active_route: str = "/admin/dashboard",
    collapsed: bool = False,
    on_toggle=None,
) -> ft.Container:
    """Barra lateral administrativa con soporte para modo colapsado y expandido."""

    items_menu = [
        ("Dashboard", "/admin/dashboard", ft.Icons.DASHBOARD_OUTLINED),
        ("Alumnos", "/admin/alumnos", ft.Icons.PEOPLE_OUTLINE),
        ("Profesores", "/admin/profesores", ft.Icons.WORK_OUTLINE),
        ("Niveles Educativos", "/admin/niveles", ft.Icons.SCHOOL_OUTLINED),
        ("Cursos", "/admin/cursos", ft.Icons.FOLDER_OPEN_OUTLINED),
        ("Materias", "/admin/materias", ft.Icons.MENU_BOOK_OUTLINED),
        ("Deportes", "/admin/deportes", ft.Icons.FITNESS_CENTER_OUTLINED),
        ("Horarios", "/admin/horarios", ft.Icons.ACCESS_TIME_OUTLINED),
        ("Inscripciones", "/admin/inscripciones", ft.Icons.DESCRIPTION_OUTLINED),
        ("Comunidad", "/admin/comunidad", ft.Icons.FORUM_OUTLINED),
        ("Transporte", "/admin/transporte", ft.Icons.DIRECTIONS_BUS_OUTLINED),
        ("Comedor", "/admin/comedor", ft.Icons.RESTAURANT_OUTLINED),
        ("Usuarios y Permisos", "/admin/usuarios", ft.Icons.LOCK_OUTLINE),
        ("Reportes", "/admin/reportes", ft.Icons.BAR_CHART_OUTLINED),
    ]

    def _cerrar_sesion(_):
        """Cierra la sesión del usuario: borra el token y vuelve al login."""
        page.auth_token = None
        page.auth_rol = None
        page.auth_user = None
        api_client.clear_token()
        page.go("/portal")

    ancho_actual = 72 if collapsed else SIDEBAR_WIDTH

    def item_sidebar(texto: str, ruta: str, icono: ft.IconData) -> ft.Container:
        es_activo = active_route == ruta

        if collapsed:
            # Modo compacto: Icono centrado de 42x42 px con tooltip descriptivo
            return ft.Container(
                data=ruta,
                height=42,
                width=42,
                border_radius=8,
                tooltip=texto,
                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT if es_activo else "transparent",
                on_click=lambda _: page.go(ruta),
                content=ft.Icon(
                    icono,
                    size=20,
                    color=TEAL_DARK if es_activo else MUTED,
                ),
            )

        # Modo extendido: Icono + Nombre + Indicador de ruta activa
        return ft.Container(
            data=ruta,
            height=42,
            border_radius=8,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 12, 16, 12),
            bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT if es_activo else "transparent",
            on_click=lambda _: page.go(ruta),
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Icon(icono, size=18, color=TEAL_DARK if es_activo else MUTED),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            texto,
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600
                            if es_activo
                            else ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=TEAL_DARK if es_activo else TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=4,
                        height=16,
                        border_radius=2,
                        bgcolor=TEAL if es_activo else "transparent",
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    # Cabecera con adaptación vertical cuando está colapsado para que el botón no se recorte
    if collapsed:
        brand_section = ft.Column(
            horizontal_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            spacing=8,
            controls=[
                ft.Container(
                    width=36,
                    height=36,
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                    border_radius=18,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    tooltip="Educar SGE",
                    content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SCHOOL, color="white", size=18),
                ),
                ft.IconButton(
                    icon=ft.Icons.MENU,
                    icon_size=20,
                    icon_color=TEAL_DARK,
                    tooltip="Expandir menú lateral",
                    on_click=on_toggle,
                ),
            ],
        )
    else:
        brand_section = ft.Row(
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Row(
                    spacing=10,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Container(
                            width=36,
                            height=36,
                            bgcolor=TEAL,
                            border_radius=18,
                            alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                            content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SCHOOL, color="white", size=18),
                        ),
                        ft.Column(
                            spacing=1,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Text(
                                    "Educar SGE",
                                    size=15,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                                ),
                                ft.Text(
                                    "GESTIÓN",
                                    size=10,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                    color=MUTED,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
                ft.IconButton(
                    icon=ft.Icons.MENU_OPEN,
                    icon_size=18,
                    icon_color=TEAL_DARK,
                    tooltip="Contraer menú lateral",
                    on_click=on_toggle,
                ),
            ],
        )

    nav_list = ft.Column(
        spacing=6,
        scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
        expand=True,
        horizontal_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER
        if collapsed
        else ft.CrossAxisAlignment.START,
        controls=[
            item_sidebar(texto, ruta, icono) for texto, ruta, icono in items_menu
        ],
    )

    if collapsed:
        footer_section = ft.Column(
            spacing=8,
            horizontal_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.IconButton(
                    icon=ft.Icons.LOCK_RESET,
                    icon_size=18,
                    icon_color=TEAL,
                    tooltip="Cambiar mi contraseña",
                    on_click=lambda _: abrir_cambio_password(page),
                ),
                ft.IconButton(
                    icon=ft.Icons.LOGOUT,
                    icon_size=18,
                    icon_color="#EF4444",
                    tooltip="Cerrar sesión",
                    on_click=_cerrar_sesion,
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    tooltip="Servidor Online",
                    content=ft.Container(
                        width=8,
                        height=8,
                        bgcolor=ONLINE_GREEN,
                        border_radius=4,
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        )
    else:
        footer_section = ft.Column(
            spacing=4,
            controls=[
                ft.Container(
                    height=36,
                    border_radius=8,
                    padding=ft.Padding(8, 6, 8, 6),
                    on_click=lambda _: abrir_cambio_password(page),
                    content=ft.Row(
                        spacing=8,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Icon(ft.Icons.LOCK_RESET, size=15, color=TEAL),
                            ft.Text("Cambiar Contraseña", size=12, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                        ],
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    height=36,
                    border_radius=8,
                    padding=ft.Padding(8, 6, 8, 6),
                    on_click=_cerrar_sesion,
                    content=ft.Row(
                        spacing=8,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Icon(ft.Icons.LOGOUT, size=15, color="#EF4444"),
                            ft.Text("Cerrar Sesión", size=12, color="#EF4444"),
                        ],
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1, thickness=1),
                ft.Row(
                    spacing=8,
                    vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Container(
                            width=8, height=8, bgcolor=ONLINE_GREEN, border_radius=4
                        ),
                        ft.Text("Servidor Online", size=12, color=MUTED),
                    ],
                ),
                ft.Text("v2.5.0-resistencia", size=10, color=MUTED),
            ],
        )

    return ft.Container(
        width=ancho_actual,
        animate_size=ft.Animation(200, ft.AnimationCurve.EASE_IN_OUT),
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border(right=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
        padding=ft.Padding(12 if collapsed else 16, 20, 12 if collapsed else 16, 20),
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=16,
            horizontal_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER
            if collapsed
            else ft.CrossAxisAlignment.START,
            controls=[
                brand_section,
                ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1, thickness=1),
                nav_list,
                ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1, thickness=1),
                footer_section,
            ],
        ),
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\components\table_view.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)


def create_table_header(columns: list[tuple[str, int | None, bool]]) -> ft.Container:
    """
    Genera la cabecera estilizada de la tabla.
    columns recibe una lista de tuplas: (nombre_columna, ancho_px, alinear_a_la_derecha)
    """
    controls = []
    for titulo, width, align_right in columns:
        alignment = ft.Alignment(1, 0) if align_right else ft.Alignment(-1, 0)
        controls.append(
            ft.Container(
                width=width,
                expand=width is None,
                alignment=alignment,
                content=ft.Text(
                    titulo,
                    size=13,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                ),
            )
        )

    return ft.Container(
        height=48,
        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
        padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=controls,
        ),
    )


# frontend/app/components/table_view.py


def create_table_container(
    header: ft.Container,
    rows: list[ft.Control],
    min_width: int = 1120,
) -> ft.Container:
    """
    Envuelve la tabla con tarjeta blanca y garantiza scroll horizontal
    activo en cualquier pantalla o nivel de zoom.
    """
    return ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        clip_behavior=ft.ClipBehavior.ANTI_ALIAS,
        content=ft.Row(
            scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
            spacing=0,
            controls=[
                ft.Container(
                    width=min_width,  # <--- Activa el cálculo de desbordamiento horizontal
                    content=ft.Column(
                        spacing=0,
                        controls=[
                            header,
                            *rows,
                        ],
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )


def create_pagination_bar(
    texto_info: str = "Mostrando registros",
    pagina_actual: int = 1,
    total_paginas: int = 3,
    on_page_change=None,
) -> ft.Row:
    """Genera la barra de paginación inferior unificada."""

    def btn_pagina(
        texto: str, activo: bool = False, icon: ft.IconData | None = None
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            width=36,
            height=36,
            bgcolor=TEAL if activo else BG_WHITE,
            border=None if activo else ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=8,
            alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
            on_click=on_page_change,
            content=ft.Icon(icon, size=16, color=TEXT_COLOR)
            if icon
            else ft.Text(
                texto,
                size=14,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600 if activo else ft.FontWeight.W_400,
                color=BG_WHITE if activo else TEXT_COLOR,
            ),
        )

    botones_paginas = [
        btn_pagina("", icon=ft.Icons.CHEVRON_LEFT),
    ]

    for p in range(1, total_paginas + 1):
        botones_paginas.append(btn_pagina(str(p), activo=(p == pagina_actual)))

    botones_paginas.append(btn_pagina("", icon=ft.Icons.CHEVRON_RIGHT))

    return ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Text(texto_info, size=14, color=MUTED),
            ft.Row(
                spacing=8,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=botones_paginas,
            ),
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\components\topbar.py ---

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.components.password_dialog import abrir_cambio_password

# Paleta y tokens visuales del CSS
BG_WHITE = "#FFFFFF"
BG_SEARCH = "#F5F7F7"
BORDER_COLOR = "#EAE6E1"
TEAL_DARK = "#0B4F4F"
TEAL = "#0D6E6E"
TEAL_LIGHT = "#E6F2F2"
TEXT_COLOR = "#1E2A29"
MUTED = "#5C6B69"


def create_topbar(
    page: ft.Page,
    titulo: str = "Tablero Principal de Control",
    usuario_nombre: str = "M. Estela Gómez",
    usuario_rol: str = "Supervisora General",
    avatar_url: str = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1573496359142-b8d87734a5a2?w=100&h=100&fit=crop",
    on_search=None,
    on_help=None,
    on_notifications=None,
) -> ft.Container:
    """Genera la barra superior (TopBar) del panel interno de control Educar SGE."""

    # Nombre y rol reales del usuario logueado (si están disponibles en la página)
    auth_user = getattr(page, "auth_user", None)
    if auth_user:
        usuario_nombre = auth_user
    rol_legible = {
        "ADMIN": "Administración",
        "TEACHER": "Docente",
        "DOCENTE": "Docente",
        "PARENT": "Tutor responsable",
        "FAMILIA": "Tutor responsable",
        "STUDENT": "Estudiante",
        "ALUMNO": "Estudiante",
    }.get((getattr(page, "auth_rol", "") or "").upper())
    if rol_legible:
        usuario_rol = rol_legible

    # 1. Buscador estilo píldora (195px x 26px aprox)
    search_input = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar legajo, DNI o curso...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=12, color=MUTED),
        text_size=12,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 8),
        expand=True,
        on_submit=on_search,
    )

    search_bar = ft.Container(
        width=195,
        height=32,
        bgcolor=BG_SEARCH,
        border_radius=100,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 4, 12, 4),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=14, color=MUTED),
                search_input,
            ],
        ),
    )

    # 2. Botón circular de ayuda (36x36px, fondo #E6F2F2)
    help_button = ft.Container(
        width=36,
        height=36,
        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
        border_radius=18,
        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
        tooltip="Centro de Ayuda",
        on_click=on_help,
        content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.HELP_OUTLINE, size=18, color=TEAL),
    )

    # 3. Botón circular de notificaciones (36x36px, fondo #E6F2F2)
    notifications_button = ft.Container(
        width=36,
        height=36,
        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
        border_radius=18,
        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
        tooltip="Notificaciones",
        on_click=on_notifications,
        content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.NOTIFICATIONS_NONE_OUTLINED, size=18, color=TEAL),
    )

    # 3b. Botón circular para cambiar la contraseña del usuario
    password_button = ft.Container(
        width=36,
        height=36,
        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
        border_radius=18,
        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
        tooltip="Cambiar mi contraseña",
        on_click=lambda _: abrir_cambio_password(page),
        content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.LOCK_RESET, size=18, color=TEAL),
    )

    # 4. Sección de Usuario (Avatar 36x36px + Nombre y Cargo)
    user_avatar = ft.Container(
        width=36,
        height=36,
        border_radius=18,
        clip_behavior=ft.ClipBehavior.ANTI_ALIAS,
        content=ft.Image(
            src=avatar_url,
            fit="cover",
            width=36,
            height=36,
        ),
    )

    user_info = ft.Column(
        spacing=1,
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Text(
                usuario_nombre,
                size=13,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                color=TEXT_COLOR,
            ),
            ft.Text(
                usuario_rol,
                size=10,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_400,
                color=MUTED,
            ),
        ],
    )

    user_section = ft.Row(
        spacing=10,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[user_avatar, user_info],
    )

    # Contenedor derecho agrupado (gap: 16px)
    acciones_derecha = ft.Row(
        spacing=16,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            search_bar,
            help_button,
            notifications_button,
            password_button,
            user_section,
        ],
    )

    # Barra principal (alto: 70px, padding horizontal: 32px)
    return ft.Container(
        height=70,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
        padding=ft.Padding.symmetric(horizontal=32),
        content=ft.Row(
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(
                    titulo,
                    size=22,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                ),
                acciones_derecha,
            ],
        ),
    )
