======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_alumnos.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import status_badge
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


async def get_admin_alumnos_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Retorna la vista completa del directorio de alumnos consumiendo asincrónicamente la API."""

    # Asegurar token desde atributos de page si api_client no lo tiene
    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    # ==================== 1. BARRA DE FILTROS Y ACCIÓN ====================
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                ft.TextField(
                    hint_text="Buscar por apellido o legajo...",
                    hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
                    text_size=14,
                    color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
                    content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
                    expand=True,
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    def filtro_dropdown(hint: str, width: int, opciones: list[str]) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            width=width,
            height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            content=ft.Dropdown(
                hint_text=hint,
                hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                text_size=14,
                border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
                content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
                options=[ft.dropdown.Option(opc) for opc in opciones],
            ),
        )

    btn_nuevo_alumno = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Alumno", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
        on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/alumnos/nuevo"),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    search_input,
                    filtro_dropdown(
                        "Nivel: Todos",
                        140,
                        [
                            "Todos",
                            "Nivel Inicial",
                            "Nivel Primario",
                            "Nivel Secundario",
                        ],
                    ),
                    filtro_dropdown(
                        "Curso: Todos",
                        140,
                        ["Todos", "Sala 5", "1er Grado", "4to Grado", "5to Año"],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
            btn_nuevo_alumno,
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 2. LLAMADA ASÍNCRONA AL BACKEND ====================
    alumnos_data: list[dict] = []
    error_servidor = False

    try:
        alumnos_data = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=True)
    except (httpx.RequestError, httpx.HTTPStatusError):
        error_servidor = True

    columnas_alumnos = [
        ("Legajo", 90, False),
        ("DNI", 110, False),
        ("Apellido", 130, False),
        ("Nombre", 140, False),
        ("Curso", 120, False),
        ("Teléfono", 120, False),
        ("Correo Electrónico", 210, False),
        ("Estado", 95, False),
        ("Acciones", 80, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_alumnos)

    def alumno_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        legajo = str(item.get("legajo", ""))
        dni = str(item.get("dni", ""))
        apellido = item.get("apellido", "")
        nombre = item.get("nombre", "")
        curso = str(item.get("curso") or item.get("id_curso") or "Sin Asignar")
        telefono = item.get("telefono") or "-"
        correo = item.get("correo_electronico") or item.get("email") or "-"

        estado_raw = item.get("estado")
        if isinstance(estado_raw, bool):
            estado_texto = "Activo" if estado_raw else "Inactivo"
        elif estado_raw:
            estado_texto = str(estado_raw)
        else:
            estado_texto = "Activo"

        async def eliminar_alumno(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_alumno(int(legajo))
                page.go("/admin/alumnos")
            except Exception as err:
                print(f"Error al eliminar alumno {legajo}: {err}")

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=90,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            legajo, size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=TEAL_DARK
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110, content=ft.Text(dni, size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=130,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            apellido,
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=140, content=ft.Text(nombre, size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120, content=ft.Text(curso, size=13, color=TEAL)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120, content=ft.Text(telefono, size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=210, content=ft.Text(correo, size=13, color=MUTED)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(width=95, content=status_badge(estado_texto)),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=80,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=8,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.EDIT_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Editar Alumno",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda _: None,
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                    tooltip="Dar de baja",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=eliminar_alumno,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    if error_servidor:
        filas_tabla = [
            ft.Container(
                padding=24,
                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                content=ft.Text(
                    "⚠️ No se pudo sincronizar con el servidor backend (FastAPI en http://127.0.0.1:8000). Verifique que esté ejecutándose.",
                    size=13,
                    color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                ),
            )
        ]
        info_paginador = "Error de sincronización con la API"
    elif not alumnos_data:
        filas_tabla = [
            ft.Container(
                padding=24,
                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                content=ft.Text(
                    "No se encontraron alumnos registrados en la base de datos.",
                    size=13,
                    color=MUTED,
                ),
            )
        ]
        info_paginador = "Mostrando 0 registros"
    else:
        filas_tabla = [alumno_row(a) for a in alumnos_data]
        info_paginador = (
            f"Mostrando {len(alumnos_data)} alumnos registrados en la base de datos"
        )

    table_container = create_table_container(
        header=header_table,
        rows=filas_tabla,
        min_width=1095,
    )

    pagination_bar = create_pagination_bar(
        texto_info=info_paginador,
        pagina_actual=1,
        total_paginas=1,
    )

    # ==================== 3. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Directorio de Estudiantes",
        active_route="/admin/alumnos",
        content_controls=[
            filters_bar,
            table_container,
            pagination_bar,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_alumno_form.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_PAGE,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)


async def get_admin_alumno_form_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Retorna la vista asíncrona de alta de alumno conectada con la API."""

    # ==================== 1. CARGA INICIAL DE CURSOS ====================
    cursos_opciones: list[ft.dropdown.Option] = []
    try:
        cursos_data = await api_client.get_cursos()
        for c in cursos_data:
            id_curso = str(c.get("id_curso", ""))
            nombre = c.get("nombre", "")
            division = c.get("division", "")
            texto = f"{nombre} {division}".strip()
            cursos_opciones.append(ft.dropdown.Option(key=id_curso, text=texto))
    except (httpx.RequestError, httpx.HTTPStatusError):
        cursos_opciones = []

    # ==================== 2. CAMPOS DEL FORMULARIO ====================
    legajo_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Autogenerado o manual (ej: 10255)",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_PAGE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    dni_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Ej: 46124990",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    nombres_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Ej: Sofía Valentina",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    apellidos_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Ej: Díaz",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    fecha_nac_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="AAAA-MM-DD (ej: 2012-05-18)",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    domicilio_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Av. Sarmiento 450, Resistencia",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    telefono_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="3624-XXXXXX",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    email_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="contacto@familia.com",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 10, 12, 10),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    curso_dropdown = ft.Dropdown(
        hint_text="Seleccione un curso...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=13, color=MUTED),
        text_size=13,
        options=cursos_opciones,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border_color=BORDER_COLOR,
        focused_border_color=TEAL,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    )

    feedback_text = ft.Text("", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, visible=False)

    def envolver_campo(
        label: str, control: ft.Control, expand: bool = True
    ) -> ft.Column:
        return ft.Column(
            spacing=6,
            expand=expand,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(label, size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                control,
            ],
        )

    # ==================== 3. LÓGICA DE GUARDADO ====================
    btn_guardar = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Text(
            "Guardar Ficha", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=37,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(20, 10, 20, 10),
        ),
    )

    async def guardar_alumno(e):
        feedback_text.visible = False
        page.update()

        dni = (dni_field.value or "").strip()
        nombres = (nombres_field.value or "").strip()
        apellidos = (apellidos_field.value or "").strip()
        legajo = (legajo_field.value or "").strip()
        fecha_nac = (fecha_nac_field.value or "").strip()
        id_curso = curso_dropdown.value

        if not dni or not nombres or not apellidos:
            feedback_text.value = "⚠️ DNI, Nombres y Apellidos son campos obligatorios."
            feedback_text.color = BADGE_RED_TXT
            feedback_text.visible = True
            page.update()
            return

        payload = {
            "dni": dni,
            "nombre": nombres,
            "apellido": apellidos,
            "domicilio": (domicilio_field.value or "").strip() or None,
            "telefono": (telefono_field.value or "").strip() or None,
            "correo_electronico": (email_field.value or "").strip() or None,
        }

        if legajo:
            try:
                payload["legajo"] = int(legajo)
            except ValueError:
                payload["legajo"] = legajo

        if fecha_nac:
            payload["fecha_nacimiento"] = fecha_nac

        if id_curso:
            try:
                payload["id_curso"] = int(id_curso)
            except ValueError:
                payload["id_curso"] = id_curso

        btn_guardar.disabled = True
        btn_guardar.content = ft.Text(
            "Guardando...", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
        )
        page.update()

        try:
            await api_client.create_alumno(payload)
            page.go("/admin/alumnos")
        except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
            btn_guardar.disabled = False
            btn_guardar.content = ft.Text(
                "Guardar Ficha", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
            )
            detalle = "Error al crear el alumno."
            try:
                data_err = err.response.json()
                detalle = data_err.get("detail", detalle)
            except Exception:
                pass
            feedback_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
            feedback_text.color = BADGE_RED_TXT
            feedback_text.visible = True
            page.update()
        except httpx.RequestError:
            btn_guardar.disabled = False
            btn_guardar.content = ft.Text(
                "Guardar Ficha", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
            )
            feedback_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
            feedback_text.color = BADGE_RED_TXT
            feedback_text.visible = True
            page.update()

    btn_guardar.on_click = guardar_alumno

    # ==================== 4. ACCIONES SUPERIORES ====================
    header_actions = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=36,
                        height=36,
                        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                        border_radius=18,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                        tooltip="Volver al listado",
                        on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/alumnos"),
                        content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ARROW_BACK, size=16, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        "Nuevo Alumno (Ciclo Lectivo)",
                        size=18,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                        color=TEAL_DARK,
                    ),
                ],
            ),
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    feedback_text,
                    ft.Button(
                        content=ft.Text(
                            "Cancelar", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=MUTED
                        ),
                        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                        height=37,
                        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                            side=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
                            padding=ft.Padding(20, 10, 20, 10),
                        ),
                        on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/alumnos"),
                    ),
                    btn_guardar,
                ],
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 5. COLUMNAS Y FORMULARIO ====================
    card_info_personal = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=20,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(
                    "Información Personal",
                    size=18,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                ),
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=16,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[
                                envolver_campo("Legajo", legajo_field),
                                envolver_campo("DNI *", dni_field),
                            ],
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[
                                envolver_campo("Nombres *", nombres_field),
                                envolver_campo("Apellidos *", apellidos_field),
                            ],
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[
                                envolver_campo("Fecha de Nacimiento", fecha_nac_field),
                                envolver_campo("Domicilio Real", domicilio_field),
                            ],
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[
                                envolver_campo("Teléfono de Contacto", telefono_field),
                                envolver_campo("Correo Electrónico Tutor", email_field),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    card_info_academica = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=20,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(
                    "Información Académica",
                    size=18,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                ),
                ft.Row(
                    spacing=16,
                    controls=[
                        envolver_campo("Curso Asignado *", curso_dropdown),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    columna_izquierda = ft.Column(
        expand=True,
        spacing=24,
        controls=[card_info_personal, card_info_academica],
    )

    # Lateral informativo
    card_instrucciones = ft.Container(
        width=360,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=16,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(
                    "Pautas de Matriculación",
                    size=17,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                ),
                ft.Text(
                    "1. El DNI debe ingresarse sin puntos ni caracteres especiales.\n\n"
                    "2. La fecha de nacimiento sigue el formato estándar AAAA-MM-DD.\n\n"
                    "3. El curso asignará automáticamente las materias y turnos predeterminados.",
                    size=13,
                    color=MUTED,
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                    border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                    padding=14,
                    content=ft.Row(
                        spacing=10,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Icon(ft.Icons.INFO_OUTLINE, size=18, color=TEAL),
                            ft.Text(
                                "Los legajos vacíos se autogeneran según la secuencia institucional.",
                                size=12,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Ficha de Alumno — Alta de Matrícula K-12",
        active_route="/admin/alumnos",
        content_controls=[
            header_actions,
            ft.Row(
                spacing=24,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.START,
                controls=[columna_izquierda, card_instrucciones],
            ),
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_comedor.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import status_badge
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


def _fecha_corta(iso: str | None) -> str:
    """Convierte un ISO timestamp del backend en dd/mm/aaaa."""
    if not iso or len(iso) < 10:
        return "-"
    return f"{iso[8:10]}/{iso[5:7]}/{iso[0:4]}"


def _nombre_alumno(valor: str) -> str:
    """Quita el sufijo '(#legajo)' para mostrar el nombre simple en la columna Estudiante."""
    texto = (valor or "").strip()
    if " (#" in texto:
        texto = texto.rsplit(" (#", 1)[0].strip()
    return texto or "-"


async def get_admin_comedor_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista de Inscripción y Control del Servicio de Comedor conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    # ==================== 1. ESTADO DE LA VISTA ====================
    estado: dict = {"inscripciones": [], "texto": "", "estado_inscripcion": "Todos"}

    try:
        estado["inscripciones"] = await api_client.get_inscripciones_comedor()
    except Exception:
        estado["inscripciones"] = []

    def esta_activo(item: dict) -> bool:
        return (item.get("estado") or "").strip().upper() == "ACTIVO"

    # ==================== 2. ENCABEZADO ====================
    encabezado_seccion = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Column(
                spacing=6,
                expand=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "Gestión de Servicios Complementarios y Nutrición",
                        size=12,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.W_400,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        "Inscripción al Servicio de Comedor Escolar",
                        size=26,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                        color=TEAL_DARK,
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        "Registre el alta de los estudiantes al comedor institucional, consulte los inscriptos y gestione las bajas del servicio.",
                        size=14,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                ],
            ),
            ft.Container(
                padding=ft.Padding(8, 4, 8, 4),
                bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                border_radius=4,
                content=ft.Text(
                    "Servicio Activo", size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=TEAL
                ),
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 3. BARRA DE FILTROS Y ACCIÓN ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar alumno o legajo...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    filtro_estado_container = ft.Container(
        width=170,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content=ft.Dropdown(
            hint_text="Estado: Todos",
            hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
            text_size=14,
            border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
            content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(opc) for opc in ["Todos", "Activo", "Inactivo"]
            ],
        ),
    )
    filtro_estado_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_estado_container.content

    btn_inscribir = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.PERSON_ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Inscribir Alumno",
                    size=14,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[search_input, filtro_estado_container],
            ),
            btn_inscribir,
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 4. TABLA DE INSCRIPTOS ====================
    columnas_comedor = [
        ("Estudiante", 260, False),
        ("Legajo", 110, False),
        ("Fecha de Alta", 140, False),
        ("Estado", 110, False),
        ("Acciones", None, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_comedor)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def inscripto_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        activo = esta_activo(item)

        async def dar_de_baja(e):
            try:
                await api_client.baja_comedor(item["legajo_alumno"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo dar de baja la inscripción al comedor."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                mostrar_error(detalle)
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                mostrar_error("Error de conexión con el backend.")
                return
            await recargar()

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=260,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _nombre_alumno(item.get("alumno", "")),
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            f"#{item['legajo_alumno']}",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=140,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _fecha_corta(item.get("fecha_alta")), size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=status_badge("Activo" if activo else "Inactivo"),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=12,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT
                                    if activo
                                    else BORDER_COLOR,
                                    tooltip="Baja del Comedor"
                                    if activo
                                    else "Inscripción ya dada de baja",
                                    padding=0,
                                    disabled=not activo,
                                    on_click=dar_de_baja if activo else None,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def inscriptos_filtrados() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").lower()
        resultado = []
        for i in estado["inscripciones"]:
            estado_legible = "Activo" if esta_activo(i) else "Inactivo"
            if (
                estado["estado_inscripcion"] != "Todos"
                and estado_legible != estado["estado_inscripcion"]
            ):
                continue
            if (
                texto
                and texto
                not in f"{i.get('alumno', '')} {i.get('legajo_alumno', '')}".lower()
            ):
                continue
            resultado.append(i)
        return resultado

    def render():
        visibles = inscriptos_filtrados()
        activos_totales = len([i for i in estado["inscripciones"] if esta_activo(i)])

        if not estado["inscripciones"]:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay alumnos inscriptos al comedor todavía."
        else:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay inscriptos que coincidan con los filtros."

        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[inscripto_row(i) for i in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(mensaje_vacio, size=13, color=MUTED),
                )
            ],
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=(
                f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['inscripciones'])} inscriptos "
                f"· {activos_totales} con servicio activo"
            ),
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["inscripciones"] = await api_client.get_inscripciones_comedor()
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de inscriptos: {err}")
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    def on_filtrar_estado(e):
        estado["estado_inscripcion"] = filtro_estado_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar
    filtro_estado_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_estado

    # ==================== 5. DIÁLOGO INSCRIBIR ALUMNO ====================
    async def abrir_inscribir_alumno(e):
        try:
            alumnos = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=True)
        except Exception:
            alumnos = []

        alumno_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Alumno *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(
                    key=str(a["legajo"]),
                    text=f"{a.get('apellido', '')}, {a.get('nombre', '')} (#{a['legajo']})",
                )
                for a in alumnos
            ],
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        if not alumnos:
            error_text.value = "⚠️ No hay alumnos activos disponibles para inscribir."
            error_text.visible = True

        async def guardar(e):
            if not alumno_field.value:
                error_text.value = "Debe seleccionar un alumno."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.inscribir_comedor(
                    {"legajo_alumno": int(alumno_field.value)}
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al inscribir al alumno en el comedor."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                "Inscribir Alumno al Comedor", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "El alta quedará registrada con la fecha del día y habilitará la vianda institucional.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    alumno_field,
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button("Inscribir", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_inscribir.on_click = abrir_inscribir_alumno

    # ==================== 6. AVISO OPERATIVO ====================
    aviso_servicio = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=14,
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.START,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.RESTAURANT_OUTLINED, size=18, color=TEAL),
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=4,
                    expand=True,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Text(
                            "Registro del Servicio de Comedor",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                        ft.Text(
                            "Las altas se registran con la fecha del día. Las bajas conservan el historial del alumno y pasan su estado a Inactivo.",
                            size=12,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    render()

    # ==================== 7. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Comedor — Inscripción al servicio",
        active_route="/admin/comedor",
        spacing=20,
        content_controls=[
            encabezado_seccion,
            filters_bar,
            aviso_error,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
            aviso_servicio,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_comunidad.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_GREEN_BG,
    BADGE_GREEN_TXT,
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BADGE_YELLOW_BG,
    BADGE_YELLOW_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.table_view import create_table_header


def _fecha_legible(iso: str | None) -> str:
    """Convierte una fecha ISO al formato dd/mm/aaaa."""
    if not iso:
        return "—"
    partes = str(iso)[:10].split("-")
    if len(partes) == 3:
        return f"{partes[2]}/{partes[1]}/{partes[0]}"
    return str(iso)


def _fecha_hora_legible(iso: str | None) -> str:
    """Convierte un datetime ISO al formato dd/mm/aaaa hh:mm."""
    if not iso:
        return "—"
    texto = str(iso)
    fecha = _fecha_legible(texto)
    if "T" in texto:
        hora = texto.split("T", 1)[1][:5]
        return f"{fecha} {hora}"
    return fecha


def _badge(texto: str, bg: str, color: str) -> ft.Container:
    return ft.Container(
        padding=ft.Padding(8, 4, 8, 4),
        bgcolor=bg,
        border_radius=4,
        content=ft.Text(texto, size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=color),
    )


def _estrellas(cantidad: int) -> ft.Row:
    return ft.Row(
        spacing=2,
        controls=[
            ft.Icon(
                ft.Icons.STAR,
                size=14,
                color="#F59E0B" if i < (cantidad or 0) else BORDER_COLOR,
            )
            for i in range(5)
        ],
    )


async def get_admin_comunidad_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Bandeja de entrada de la comunidad: mensajes de contacto y moderación de opiniones."""

    token = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token)

    mensajes: list[dict] = []
    opiniones: list[dict] = []
    error_carga = None
    try:
        mensajes = await api_client.get_mensajes_contacto()
    except Exception:
        error_carga = "No se pudieron cargar los mensajes de contacto."
    try:
        opiniones = await api_client.get_opiniones_todas()
    except Exception:
        error_carga = (
            error_carga + " " if error_carga else ""
        ) + "No se pudieron cargar las opiniones."

    # ==================== HELPERS DE UI ====================
    def tarjeta(titulo: str, subtitulo: str, contenido: ft.Control) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
            padding=20,
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=12,
                controls=[
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=2,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                titulo,
                                size=16,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(subtitulo, size=12, color=MUTED),
                        ],
                    ),
                    contenido,
                ],
            ),
        )

    def tabla(filas: ft.Column) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            clip_behavior=ft.ClipBehavior.ANTI_ALIAS,
            content=ft.Row(
                scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
                spacing=0,
                controls=[ft.Container(width=1150, content=filas)],
            ),
        )

    def sin_datos(texto: str) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            padding=24,
            alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
            content=ft.Text(texto, size=13, color=MUTED),
        )

    # ==================== 1. MENSAJES DE CONTACTO ====================
    header_mensajes = create_table_header(
        [
            ("Remitente", 150, False),
            ("Correo", 190, False),
            ("Teléfono", 130, False),
            ("Mensaje", None, False),
            ("Fecha", 140, False),
            ("Estado", 90, False),
            ("Acciones", 100, True),
        ]
    )
    filas_mensajes = ft.Column(spacing=0, controls=[header_mensajes])

    async def marcar_leido(e, mensaje: dict):
        try:
            await api_client.marcar_mensaje_leido(mensaje["id_mensaje"])
        except Exception:
            page.show_dialog(
                ft.SnackBar(
                    ft.Text("No se pudo marcar el mensaje como leído.", color="white"),
                    bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                )
            )
            return
        mensaje["leido"] = True
        render_mensajes()
        page.show_dialog(
            ft.SnackBar(
                ft.Text("Mensaje marcado como leído.", color="white"), bgcolor=TEAL
            )
        )

    def ver_mensaje(e, mensaje: dict):
        dialogo = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Mensaje de {mensaje.get('nombre') or 'Anónimo'}",
                size=16,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                color=TEAL_DARK,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=10,
                width=440,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"Correo: {mensaje.get('correo') or '—'}",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        f"Teléfono: {mensaje.get('telefono') or '—'}",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        f"Recibido: {_fecha_hora_legible(mensaje.get('fecha'))}",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1),
                    ft.Text(mensaje.get("mensaje") or "", size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                ],
            ),
            actions=[ft.TextButton("Cerrar", on_click=lambda _: page.pop_dialog())],
            actions_alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
        )
        page.show_dialog(dialogo)

    def fila_mensaje(mensaje: dict) -> ft.Container:
        leido = bool(mensaje.get("leido"))
        badge = (
            _badge("Leído", BADGE_GREEN_BG, BADGE_GREEN_TXT)
            if leido
            else _badge("Nuevo", BADGE_YELLOW_BG, BADGE_YELLOW_TXT)
        )
        resumen = (mensaje.get("mensaje") or "").strip()
        if len(resumen) > 60:
            resumen = resumen[:57] + "..."
        return ft.Container(
            height=52,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=150,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            mensaje.get("nombre") or "—",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=190,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            mensaje.get("correo") or "—", size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=130,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            mensaje.get("telefono") or "—", size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True, content=ft.Text(resumen, size=13, color=MUTED)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=140,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _fecha_hora_legible(mensaje.get("fecha")),
                            size=12,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(width=90, content=badge),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=100,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=8,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.MARK_EMAIL_READ_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=18,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Marcar como leído",
                                    padding=0,
                                    visible=not leido,
                                    on_click=lambda e, m=mensaje: marcar_leido(e, m),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.VISIBILITY_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=18,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Ver mensaje completo",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, m=mensaje: ver_mensaje(e, m),
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def render_mensajes():
        if not mensajes:
            filas_mensajes.controls = [
                header_mensajes,
                sin_datos("No hay mensajes de contacto recibidos."),
            ]
        else:
            filas_mensajes.controls = [
                header_mensajes,
                *[fila_mensaje(m) for m in mensajes],
            ]
        page.update()

    # ==================== 2. MODERACIÓN DE OPINIONES ====================
    header_opiniones = create_table_header(
        [
            ("Autor", 150, False),
            ("Vínculo", 150, False),
            ("Comentario", None, False),
            ("Puntaje", 90, False),
            ("Fecha", 110, False),
            ("Estado", 100, False),
            ("Acciones", 100, True),
        ]
    )
    filas_opiniones = ft.Column(spacing=0, controls=[header_opiniones])

    async def aprobar_opinion(e, opinion: dict):
        try:
            await api_client.aprobar_opinion(opinion["id_opinion"])
        except Exception:
            page.show_dialog(
                ft.SnackBar(
                    ft.Text("No se pudo aprobar la opinión.", color="white"),
                    bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                )
            )
            return
        opinion["aprobada"] = True
        render_opiniones()
        page.show_dialog(
            ft.SnackBar(
                ft.Text("Opinión aprobada y publicada en el sitio.", color="white"),
                bgcolor=TEAL,
            )
        )

    def confirmar_eliminar(e, opinion: dict):
        async def eliminar_confirmado(e2):
            try:
                await api_client.eliminar_opinion(opinion["id_opinion"])
            except Exception:
                page.pop_dialog()
                page.show_dialog(
                    ft.SnackBar(
                        ft.Text("No se pudo eliminar la opinión.", color="white"),
                        bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                    )
                )
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            if opinion in opiniones:
                opiniones.remove(opinion)
            render_opiniones()
            page.show_dialog(
                ft.SnackBar(
                    ft.Text("Opinión rechazada y eliminada.", color="white"),
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                )
            )

        dialogo = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            title=ft.Text(
                "Rechazar opinión", size=16, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEAL_DARK
            ),
            content=ft.Text(
                f"¿Confirma que desea rechazar y eliminar la opinión de {opinion.get('nombre') or 'Anónimo'}?",
                size=13,
                color=TEXT_COLOR,
                width=380,
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda _: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Eliminar",
                    bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                    on_click=eliminar_confirmado,
                ),
            ],
            actions_alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
        )
        page.show_dialog(dialogo)

    def fila_opinion(opinion: dict) -> ft.Container:
        aprobada = bool(opinion.get("aprobada"))
        badge = (
            _badge("Aprobada", BADGE_GREEN_BG, BADGE_GREEN_TXT)
            if aprobada
            else _badge("Pendiente", BADGE_YELLOW_BG, BADGE_YELLOW_TXT)
        )
        comentario = (opinion.get("comentario") or "").strip()
        if len(comentario) > 60:
            comentario = comentario[:57] + "..."
        return ft.Container(
            height=52,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=150,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            opinion.get("nombre") or "—",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=150,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            opinion.get("vinculo") or "—", size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True, content=ft.Text(comentario, size=13, color=MUTED)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=90, content=_estrellas(opinion.get("estrellas"))
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _fecha_legible(opinion.get("fecha")), size=12, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(width=100, content=badge),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=100,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=8,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.THUMB_UP_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=18,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Aprobar y publicar",
                                    padding=0,
                                    visible=not aprobada,
                                    on_click=lambda e, o=opinion: aprobar_opinion(e, o),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=18,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                    tooltip="Rechazar / eliminar",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, o=opinion: confirmar_eliminar(
                                        e, o
                                    ),
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def render_opiniones():
        if not opiniones:
            filas_opiniones.controls = [
                header_opiniones,
                sin_datos("No hay opiniones registradas."),
            ]
        else:
            filas_opiniones.controls = [
                header_opiniones,
                *[fila_opinion(o) for o in opiniones],
            ]
        page.update()

    render_mensajes()
    render_opiniones()

    pendientes = sum(1 for o in opiniones if not o.get("aprobada"))
    nuevos = sum(1 for m in mensajes if not m.get("leido"))

    if error_carga:
        aviso = ft.Container(
            bgcolor="#FDE8E8",
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            padding=12,
            content=ft.Text(error_carga, size=13, color=BADGE_RED_TXT),
        )
    else:
        aviso = ft.Container()

    # ==================== 3. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Comunidad — Mensajes y Opiniones",
        active_route="/admin/comunidad",
        content_controls=[
            aviso,
            tarjeta(
                "Mensajes de Contacto",
                f"{len(mensajes)} mensajes recibidos · {nuevos} sin leer",
                tabla(filas_mensajes),
            ),
            tarjeta(
                "Moderación de Opiniones",
                f"{len(opiniones)} opiniones · {pendientes} pendientes de aprobación",
                tabla(filas_opiniones),
            ),
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_cursos.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


async def get_admin_cursos_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista del directorio de Cursos y Divisiones conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    estado: dict = {
        "cursos": [],
        "niveles": [],
        "alumnos": [],
        "texto": "",
        "nivel": "Todos",
    }

    try:
        estado["cursos"] = await api_client.get_cursos()
    except Exception:
        estado["cursos"] = []
    try:
        estado["niveles"] = await api_client.get_niveles()
    except Exception:
        estado["niveles"] = []
    try:
        estado["alumnos"] = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=False)
    except Exception:
        estado["alumnos"] = []

    nombre_nivel = {n["id_nivel"]: n["nombre"] for n in estado["niveles"]}

    # ==================== 1. BARRA DE FILTROS Y ACCIÓN ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar curso o división...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    filtro_nivel_container = ft.Container(
        width=180,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content=ft.Dropdown(
            hint_text="Nivel: Todos",
            hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
            text_size=14,
            border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
            content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
            options=[ft.dropdown.Option("Todos")]
            + [ft.dropdown.Option(n["nombre"]) for n in estado["niveles"]],
        ),
    )
    filtro_nivel_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_nivel_container.content

    btn_nuevo_curso = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Curso", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[search_input, filtro_nivel_container],
            ),
            btn_nuevo_curso,
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 2. TABLA DE CURSOS ====================
    columnas_cursos = [
        ("Código", 100, False),
        ("Curso / Año", 220, False),
        ("Nivel Educativo", 200, False),
        ("Matrícula Activa", 170, False),
        ("Total Alumnos", 160, False),
        ("Acciones", None, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_cursos)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def fila_acciones(al_editar, al_eliminar) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            expand=True,
            alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=6,
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                controls=[
                    ft.IconButton(
                        icon=ft.Icons.EDIT_OUTLINED,
                        icon_size=17,
                        icon_color=TEAL,
                        tooltip="Editar curso",
                        padding=0,
                        on_click=al_editar,
                    ),
                    ft.IconButton(
                        icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                        icon_size=17,
                        icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                        tooltip="Eliminar curso",
                        padding=0,
                        on_click=al_eliminar,
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def alumnos_del_curso(id_curso: int) -> list[dict]:
        return [a for a in estado["alumnos"] if a.get("id_curso") == id_curso]

    def curso_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        alumnos_curso = alumnos_del_curso(item["id_curso"])
        activos = len([a for a in alumnos_curso if a.get("estado_alumno") == "ACTIVO"])

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=100,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(item["id_curso"]),
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=220,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item["nombre"],
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=200,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            nombre_nivel.get(
                                item["id_nivel"], f"Nivel {item['id_nivel']}"
                            ),
                            size=14,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=170,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(activos),
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=160,
                        content=ft.Text(str(len(alumnos_curso)), size=14, color=MUTED),
                    ),
                    fila_acciones(
                        lambda e, c=item: abrir_editar_curso(c),
                        lambda e, c=item: confirmar_eliminar_curso(c),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def cursos_filtrados() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").lower()
        resultado = []
        for c in estado["cursos"]:
            nivel = nombre_nivel.get(c["id_nivel"], "")
            if estado["nivel"] != "Todos" and nivel != estado["nivel"]:
                continue
            if (
                texto
                and texto
                not in f"{c.get('nombre', '')} {c.get('id_curso', '')}".lower()
            ):
                continue
            resultado.append(c)
        return resultado

    def render():
        visibles = cursos_filtrados()
        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[curso_row(c) for c in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "No hay cursos que coincidan con los filtros.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                )
            ],
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['cursos'])} cursos activos",
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["cursos"] = await api_client.get_cursos()
            estado["alumnos"] = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=False)
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de cursos: {err}")
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    def on_filtrar_nivel(e):
        estado["nivel"] = filtro_nivel_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar
    filtro_nivel_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_nivel

    # ==================== 3. DIÁLOGO NUEVO CURSO ====================
    def abrir_nuevo_curso(e):
        if not estado["niveles"]:
            mostrar_error("No hay niveles educativos cargados. Cree un nivel primero.")
            return

        nombre_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nombre del curso *", hint_text="Ej: 2° Año B", width=380
        )
        nivel_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Nivel educativo *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(n["id_nivel"]), text=n["nombre"])
                for n in estado["niveles"]
            ],
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            nombre = (nombre_field.value or "").strip()
            if not nombre or not nivel_field.value:
                error_text.value = "Nombre y nivel son obligatorios."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.create_curso(
                    {"nombre": nombre, "id_nivel": int(nivel_field.value)}
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear el curso."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Nuevo Curso", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[nombre_field, nivel_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Curso", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nuevo_curso.on_click = abrir_nuevo_curso

    # ==================== 3b. DIÁLOGOS EDITAR / ELIMINAR CURSO ====================
    def abrir_editar_curso(curso: dict):
        nombre_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nombre del curso *",
            value=curso.get("nombre") or "",
            width=380,
        )
        nivel_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Nivel educativo *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(n["id_nivel"]), text=n["nombre"])
                for n in estado["niveles"]
            ],
            value=str(curso.get("id_nivel")),
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar_edicion(e):
            nombre = (nombre_field.value or "").strip()
            if not nombre or not nivel_field.value:
                error_text.value = "Nombre y nivel son obligatorios."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.update_curso(
                    curso["id_curso"],
                    {"nombre": nombre, "id_nivel": int(nivel_field.value)},
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al actualizar el curso."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Editar Curso #{curso['id_curso']}", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[nombre_field, nivel_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Guardar Cambios",
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                    on_click=guardar_edicion,
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    def confirmar_eliminar_curso(curso: dict):
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def eliminar(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_curso(curso["id_curso"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo eliminar el curso."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Eliminar Curso", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"¿Confirma eliminar el curso «{curso.get('nombre')}»? "
                        "Solo es posible si no tiene alumnos ni asignaciones asociadas.",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Eliminar", bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=eliminar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    render()

    # ==================== 4. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Gestión Académica — Cursos y Divisiones",
        active_route="/admin/cursos",
        content_controls=[
            filters_bar,
            aviso_error,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_dashboard.py ---

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    AMBER_STATE,
    BADGE_YELLOW_BG,
    BG_PAGE,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    MUTED,
    ONLINE_GREEN,
    ORANGE,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)


async def get_admin_dashboard_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Retorna la vista completa del panel interno Educar SGE usando AdminLayout
    con las métricas reales calculadas desde la base de datos."""

    token = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token)

    resumen = {
        "alumnos_activos": "—",
