        "docentes_activos": "—",
        "cursos": "—",
        "niveles": "—",
        "solicitudes_pendientes": "—",
        "mensajes_no_leidos": "—",
        "transporte_activos": "—",
        "comedor_activos": "—",
    }
    try:
        resumen.update(await api_client.get_resumen_dashboard())
    except Exception:
        pass

    usuario = getattr(page, "auth_user", None) or "Administración"

    # ==================== 1. BANNER INSTITUCIONAL ====================
    welcome_banner = ft.Container(
        height=110,
        bgcolor=TEAL_DARK,
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(20, 20, 20, 20),
        content=ft.Row(
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=8,
                    alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Text(
                            f"¡Buen día, {usuario}! Ciclo Lectivo Activo",
                            size=22,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                            color=BG_WHITE,
                        ),
                        ft.Text(
                            f"Hoy hay {resumen['solicitudes_pendientes']} solicitudes de admisión pendientes "
                            f"y {resumen['mensajes_no_leidos']} mensajes de contacto sin leer.",
                            size=14,
                            color=TEAL_LIGHT,
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
                ft.Button(
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "Ver Solicitudes Nuevas",
                        size=13,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                        color=BG_WHITE,
                    ),
                    bgcolor=ORANGE,
                    height=36,
                    style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                        shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
                        padding=ft.Padding(20, 10, 20, 10),
                    ),
                    on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/inscripciones"),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    # ==================== 2. TARJETAS DE MÉTRICAS (KPIS) ====================
    def kpi_card(
        titulo: str, valor: str, subtitulo: str, icono: ft.IconData, icon_bg: str
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            expand=True,
            height=158,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
            padding=20,
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=16,
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                controls=[
                    ft.Row(
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                titulo, size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=MUTED
                            ),
                            ft.Container(
                                width=36,
                                height=36,
                                bgcolor=icon_bg,
                                border_radius=10,
                                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                content=ft.Icon(icono, size=18, color=TEAL_DARK),
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=4,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                valor,
                                size=30,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(subtitulo, size=11, color=MUTED),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    kpis_row = ft.Row(
        spacing=16,
        controls=[
            kpi_card(
                "ALUMNOS MATRICULADOS",
                str(resumen["alumnos_activos"]),
                "Matrícula activa en el sistema",
                ft.Icons.PEOPLE_OUTLINE,
                TEAL_LIGHT,
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "PERSONAL DOCENTE",
                str(resumen["docentes_activos"]),
                "Plantel docente activo",
                ft.Icons.WORK_OUTLINE,
                BADGE_YELLOW_BG,
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "INSCRIPCIONES PENDIENTES",
                str(resumen["solicitudes_pendientes"]),
                "Solicitudes de admisión en secretaría",
                ft.Icons.DESCRIPTION_OUTLINED,
                TEAL_LIGHT,
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "CURSOS ACTIVOS",
                str(resumen["cursos"]),
                f"{resumen['niveles']} niveles educativos",
                ft.Icons.FOLDER_OPEN_OUTLINED,
                TEAL_LIGHT,
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 3. ACCESOS DIRECTOS COMPACTOS ====================
    def shortcut_item(
        titulo: str,
        subtitulo: str,
        icono: ft.IconData,
        bg_icon: str,
        color_icon: str,
        ruta: str,
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            height=60,
            bgcolor=BG_PAGE,
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            padding=10,
            on_click=lambda _: page.go(ruta),
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=34,
                        height=34,
                        bgcolor=bg_icon,
                        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                        content=ft.Icon(icono, size=16, color=color_icon),
                    ),
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=2,
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                titulo,
                                size=13,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                color=TEXT_COLOR,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(subtitulo, size=11, color=MUTED),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    shortcuts_card = ft.Container(
        width=380,
        height=367,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=16,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(
                    "Accesos Directos",
                    size=18,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                ),
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=10,
                    controls=[
                        shortcut_item(
                            "Registrar Nuevo Alumno",
                            "Formulario unificado K-12",
                            ft.Icons.PERSON_ADD_ALT_1_OUTLINED,
                            TEAL_LIGHT,
                            TEAL_DARK,
                            "/admin/alumnos/nuevo",
                        ),
                        shortcut_item(
                            "Materias y Cursos",
                            "Plan de estudios vigente",
                            ft.Icons.MENU_BOOK_OUTLINED,
                            BADGE_YELLOW_BG,
                            ORANGE,
                            "/admin/materias",
                        ),
                        shortcut_item(
                            "Transporte Escolar",
                            "4 recorridos oficiales",
                            ft.Icons.DIRECTIONS_BUS_OUTLINED,
                            TEAL_LIGHT,
                            ONLINE_GREEN,
                            "/admin/transporte",
                        ),
                        shortcut_item(
                            "Reportes Directivos",
                            "Listados consolidados",
                            ft.Icons.PRINT_OUTLINED,
                            BG_PAGE,
                            MUTED,
                            "/admin/reportes",
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    # ==================== 4. ACTIVIDADES (FLUIDA) ====================
    def activity_item(
        texto_bold: str,
        texto_regular: str,
        tiempo: str,
        categoria: str,
        color_linea: str,
        color_badge: str,
    ) -> ft.Row:
        return ft.Row(
            spacing=14,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Container(width=4, height=38, border_radius=2, bgcolor=color_linea),
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=3,
                    expand=True,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Text(
                            spans=[
                                ft.TextSpan(
                                    texto_bold,
                                    ft.TextStyle(
                                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEXT_COLOR
                                    ),
                                ),
                                ft.TextSpan(
                                    f" {texto_regular}", ft.TextStyle(color=TEXT_COLOR)
                                ),
                            ],
                            size=13,
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=8,
                            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Text(tiempo, size=11, color=MUTED),
                                ft.Text("•", size=11, color=MUTED),
                                ft.Text(
                                    categoria.upper(),
                                    size=10,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                    color=color_badge,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        )

    activities_card = ft.Container(
        expand=True,
        height=367,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=18,
            controls=[
                ft.Row(
                    alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Text(
                            "Últimos Movimientos del Sistema",
                            size=18,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                        ft.TextButton(
                            "Ver Historial",
                            style=ft.ButtonStyle(color=ORANGE, padding=0),
                            on_click=lambda _: None,
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=14,
                    controls=[
                        activity_item(
                            "Preceptora Secundario",
                            "Registró asistencia de 4to Año 'A'.",
                            "Hace 10 mins",
                            "Asistencia",
                            TEAL,
                            TEAL,
                        ),
                        activity_item(
                            "Administración Central",
                            "Validó la inscripción del Legajo #4928.",
                            "Hace 45 mins",
                            "Inscripción",
                            ONLINE_GREEN,
                            ONLINE_GREEN,
                        ),
                        activity_item(
                            "Secretaría Inicial",
                            "Actualizó cupos en Sala de 4 años.",
                            "Hace 2 horas",
                            "Sistema",
                            AMBER_STATE,
                            AMBER_STATE,
                        ),
                        activity_item(
                            "Prof. Martínez Román",
                            "Cargó horarios de Física y Química.",
                            "Ayer, 18:30",
                            "Docentes",
                            ORANGE,
                            ORANGE,
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    bottom_row = ft.Row(
        spacing=20,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.START,
        controls=[shortcuts_card, activities_card],
    )

    # ==================== 5. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Tablero Principal de Control",
        active_route="/admin/dashboard",
        content_controls=[
            welcome_banner,
            kpis_row,
            bottom_row,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_deportes.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    ORANGE,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


def _fecha_corta(iso: str | None) -> str:
    """Convierte un ISO timestamp del backend en dd/mm/aaaa."""
    if not iso or len(iso) < 10:
        return "-"
    return f"{iso[8:10]}/{iso[5:7]}/{iso[0:4]}"


async def get_admin_deportes_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista del programa deportivo (disciplinas, grupos e inscripciones) conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    # ==================== ESTADO DE LA VISTA ====================
    estado: dict = {
        "deportes": [],
        "grupos": [],
        "inscripciones": [],
        "profesores": [],
        "niveles": [],
        "horarios": [],
        "texto": "",
        "nivel": "Todos",
        "deporte": "Todos",
    }

    try:
        estado["deportes"] = await api_client.get_deportes()
    except Exception:
        estado["deportes"] = []
    try:
        estado["grupos"] = await api_client.get_grupos_deporte()
    except Exception:
        estado["grupos"] = []
    try:
        estado["inscripciones"] = await api_client.get_inscripciones_deporte()
    except Exception:
        estado["inscripciones"] = []
    try:
        estado["profesores"] = await api_client.get_profesores(solo_activos=False)
    except Exception:
        estado["profesores"] = []
    try:
        estado["niveles"] = await api_client.get_niveles()
    except Exception:
        estado["niveles"] = []
    try:
        estado["horarios"] = await api_client.get_horarios()
    except Exception:
        estado["horarios"] = []

    profesores_por_legajo = {p.get("legajo"): p for p in estado["profesores"]}

    def nombre_profesor(legajo) -> str:
        if legajo is None:
            return "Sin asignar"
        profesor = profesores_por_legajo.get(legajo)
        if not profesor:
            return f"Legajo {legajo}"
        return f"{profesor.get('apellido', '')}, {profesor.get('nombre', '')}"

    def profesor_de_deporte(id_deporte: int | None) -> str:
        for d in estado["deportes"]:
            if d.get("id_deporte") == id_deporte:
                return nombre_profesor(d.get("legajo_profesor_responsable"))
        return "Sin asignar"

    def inscriptos_de_grupo(id_grupo: int) -> list[dict]:
        return [i for i in estado["inscripciones"] if i.get("id_grupo") == id_grupo]

    # Opciones de filtros (si las entidades base fallan, se derivan de los grupos reales)
    nombres_nivel = [n["nombre"] for n in estado["niveles"] if n.get("nombre")]
    if not nombres_nivel:
        nombres_nivel = sorted(
            {g.get("nivel", "") for g in estado["grupos"] if g.get("nivel")}
        )
    nombres_deporte = [d["nombre"] for d in estado["deportes"] if d.get("nombre")]
    if not nombres_deporte:
        nombres_deporte = sorted(
            {g.get("deporte", "") for g in estado["grupos"] if g.get("deporte")}
        )

    # ==================== 1. TARJETAS DE MÉTRICAS (KPIs) ====================
    def kpi_card(
        titulo: str,
        valor: str,
        subtitulo: str,
        icono: ft.IconData,
        icon_bg: str,
        valor_color: str = TEAL_DARK,
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            expand=True,
            height=158,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
            padding=20,
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=16,
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                controls=[
                    ft.Row(
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                titulo, size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=MUTED
                            ),
                            ft.Container(
                                width=36,
                                height=36,
                                bgcolor=icon_bg,
                                border_radius=10,
                                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                content=ft.Icon(icono, size=18, color=TEAL_DARK),
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=4,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                valor,
                                size=30,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800,
                                color=valor_color,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(subtitulo, size=11, color=MUTED),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def render_kpis() -> ft.Row:
        con_docente = len(
            [
                d
                for d in estado["deportes"]
                if d.get("legajo_profesor_responsable") is not None
            ]
        )
        sin_docente = len(estado["deportes"]) - con_docente
        niveles_con_grupos = {
            g.get("nivel", "") for g in estado["grupos"] if g.get("nivel")
        }
        total_inscripciones = len(estado["inscripciones"])
        total_grupos = len(estado["grupos"])
        promedio = (total_inscripciones / total_grupos) if total_grupos else 0

        return ft.Row(
            spacing=16,
            controls=[
                kpi_card(
                    "DISCIPLINAS DEPORTIVAS",
                    str(len(estado["deportes"])),
                    f"{con_docente} con docente asignado",
                    ft.Icons.SPORTS_OUTLINED,
                    TEAL_LIGHT,
                ),
                kpi_card(
                    "GRUPOS DEPORTIVOS",
                    str(total_grupos),
                    f"En {len(niveles_con_grupos)} niveles educativos"
                    if niveles_con_grupos
                    else "Sin grupos registrados",
                    ft.Icons.GROUP_WORK_OUTLINED,
                    TEAL_LIGHT,
                ),
                kpi_card(
                    "ALUMNOS INSCRIPTOS",
                    str(total_inscripciones),
                    f"Promedio {promedio:.1f} por grupo"
                    if total_grupos
                    else "Sin inscripciones registradas",
                    ft.Icons.HOW_TO_REG_OUTLINED,
                    TEAL_LIGHT,
                ),
                kpi_card(
                    "DOCENTES A CARGO",
                    str(con_docente),
                    f"{sin_docente} disciplinas sin responsable"
                    if sin_docente
                    else "Todas las disciplinas cubiertas",
                    ft.Icons.SUPERVISOR_ACCOUNT_OUTLINED,
                    "#FFF3E7" if sin_docente else TEAL_LIGHT,
                    ORANGE if sin_docente else TEAL_DARK,
                ),
            ],
        )

    # ==================== 2. FILTROS Y ACCIONES ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar disciplina, nivel, horario o docente...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=320,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    def filtro_dropdown(hint: str, width: int, opciones: list[str]) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            width=width,
            height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            content=ft.Dropdown(
                hint_text=hint,
                hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                text_size=14,
                border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
                content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
                options=[ft.dropdown.Option(opc) for opc in opciones],
            ),
        )

    filtro_nivel_container = filtro_dropdown(
        "Nivel: Todos", 150, ["Todos"] + nombres_nivel
    )
    filtro_deporte_container = filtro_dropdown(
        "Disciplina: Todas", 170, ["Todos"] + nombres_deporte
    )
    filtro_nivel_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_nivel_container.content
    filtro_deporte_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_deporte_container.content

    btn_nuevo_deporte = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Deporte", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    btn_nuevo_grupo = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.GROUP_ADD_OUTLINED, size=16, color=TEAL),
                ft.Text("Nuevo Grupo", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=TEAL),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
            side=ft.BorderSide(1, TEAL),
        ),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    search_input,
                    filtro_nivel_container,
                    filtro_deporte_container,
                ],
            ),
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[btn_nuevo_grupo, btn_nuevo_deporte],
            ),
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 3. TABLA DE GRUPOS DEPORTIVOS ====================
    columnas_grupos = [
        ("Disciplina Deportiva", 190, False),
        ("Profesor Responsable", 190, False),
        ("Nivel Destinado", 150, False),
        ("Días y Horarios", 260, False),
        ("Inscriptos", 110, False),
        ("Acciones", None, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_grupos)
    kpis_holder = ft.Container()
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def grupo_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        profesor = profesor_de_deporte(item.get("id_deporte"))
        inscriptos = len(inscriptos_de_grupo(item["id_grupo"]))

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=190,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("deporte", "-"),
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=190,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            profesor,
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=MUTED if profesor == "Sin asignar" else TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=150,
                        content=ft.Text(item.get("nivel", "-"), size=13, color=MUTED),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=260,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("horario", "-"), size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(inscriptos),
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=12,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.PEOPLE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Ver Alumnos Inscriptos",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, g=item: abrir_inscriptos(g),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.EDIT_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Editar grupo",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, g=item: abrir_editar_grupo(g),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                    tooltip="Eliminar grupo",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, g=item: confirmar_eliminar_grupo(
                                        g
                                    ),
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def grupos_filtrados() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").lower()
        resultado = []
        for g in estado["grupos"]:
            if estado["nivel"] != "Todos" and g.get("nivel") != estado["nivel"]:
                continue
            if estado["deporte"] != "Todos" and g.get("deporte") != estado["deporte"]:
                continue
            if texto:
                buscable = " ".join(
                    [
                        str(g.get("deporte", "")),
                        str(g.get("nivel", "")),
                        str(g.get("horario", "")),
                        profesor_de_deporte(g.get("id_deporte")),
                        f"grupo {g.get('id_grupo', '')}",
                    ]
                ).lower()
                if texto not in buscable:
                    continue
            resultado.append(g)
        return resultado

    def render():
        visibles = grupos_filtrados()
        kpis_holder.content = render_kpis()
        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[grupo_row(g) for g in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "No hay grupos deportivos cargados."
                        if not estado["grupos"]
                        else "No hay grupos deportivos que coincidan con los filtros.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                )
            ],
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=(
                f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['grupos'])} grupos deportivos · "
                f"{len(estado['inscripciones'])} alumnos inscriptos"
            ),
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["deportes"] = await api_client.get_deportes()
            estado["grupos"] = await api_client.get_grupos_deporte()
            estado["inscripciones"] = await api_client.get_inscripciones_deporte()
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la información deportiva: {err}")
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    def on_filtrar_nivel(e):
        estado["nivel"] = filtro_nivel_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    def on_filtrar_deporte(e):
        estado["deporte"] = filtro_deporte_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar
    filtro_nivel_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_nivel
    filtro_deporte_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_deporte

    # ==================== 4. DIÁLOGO DE ALUMNOS INSCRIPTOS ====================
    def abrir_inscriptos(grupo: dict):
        inscritos = sorted(
            inscriptos_de_grupo(grupo["id_grupo"]),
            key=lambda i: str(i.get("alumno", "")),
        )

        filas = [
            ft.Container(
                padding=ft.Padding(0, 6, 0, 6),
                content=ft.Row(
                    alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                    vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Text(
                            i.get("alumno", f"Legajo {i.get('legajo_alumno', '-')}"),
                            size=13,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                        ft.Text(
                            _fecha_corta(i.get("fecha_inscripcion")),
                            size=12,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            )
            for i in inscritos
        ] or [
            ft.Text("No hay alumnos inscriptos en este grupo.", size=13, color=MUTED),
        ]

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Alumnos Inscriptos — {grupo.get('deporte', '')}",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=12,
                width=420,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"{grupo.get('nivel', '-')} · {grupo.get('horario', '-')}",
                        size=12,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    ft.ListView(spacing=0, height=240, controls=filas),
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cerrar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    # ==================== 5. DIÁLOGO NUEVO DEPORTE ====================
    def abrir_nuevo_deporte(e):
        nombre_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nombre de la disciplina *", hint_text="Ej: Natación", width=380
        )
        profesor_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Profesor responsable",
            width=380,
            options=[ft.dropdown.Option(key="", text="Sin responsable")]
            + [
                ft.dropdown.Option(
                    key=str(p["legajo"]),
                    text=f"{p.get('apellido', '')}, {p.get('nombre', '')} (#{p['legajo']})",
                )
                for p in estado["profesores"]
            ],
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            nombre = (nombre_field.value or "").strip()
            if not nombre:
                error_text.value = "El nombre de la disciplina es obligatorio."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return

            payload = {
                "nombre": nombre,
                "legajo_profesor_responsable": int(profesor_field.value)
                if profesor_field.value
                else None,
            }
            try:
                await api_client.create_deporte(payload)
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear la disciplina deportiva."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                "Nueva Disciplina Deportiva", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[nombre_field, profesor_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Deporte", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nuevo_deporte.on_click = abrir_nuevo_deporte

    # ==================== 6. DIÁLOGO NUEVO GRUPO ====================
    def abrir_nuevo_grupo(e):
        if not estado["deportes"] or not estado["niveles"] or not estado["horarios"]:
            mostrar_error(
                "Para crear un grupo se necesitan disciplinas, niveles y horarios cargados."
            )
            return

        deporte_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Disciplina deportiva *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(d["id_deporte"]), text=d["nombre"])
                for d in estado["deportes"]
            ],
        )
        nivel_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Nivel educativo *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(n["id_nivel"]), text=n["nombre"])
                for n in estado["niveles"]
            ],
        )
        horario_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Horario *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(h["id_horario"]), text=h["descripcion"])
                for h in estado["horarios"]
            ],
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            if (
                not deporte_field.value
                or not nivel_field.value
                or not horario_field.value
            ):
                error_text.value = "Disciplina, nivel y horario son obligatorios."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return

            payload = {
                "id_deporte": int(deporte_field.value),
                "id_nivel": int(nivel_field.value),
                "id_horario": int(horario_field.value),
            }
            try:
                await api_client.create_grupo_deporte(payload)
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear el grupo deportivo."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Nuevo Grupo Deportivo", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[deporte_field, nivel_field, horario_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Grupo", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nuevo_grupo.on_click = abrir_nuevo_grupo

    # ==================== 6b. DIÁLOGOS EDITAR / ELIMINAR GRUPO ====================
    def abrir_editar_grupo(grupo: dict):
        deporte_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Disciplina deportiva *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(d["id_deporte"]), text=d["nombre"])
                for d in estado["deportes"]
            ],
            value=str(grupo.get("id_deporte")),
        )
        nivel_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Nivel educativo *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(n["id_nivel"]), text=n["nombre"])
                for n in estado["niveles"]
            ],
            value=str(grupo.get("id_nivel")),
        )
        horario_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Horario *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(h["id_horario"]), text=h["descripcion"])
                for h in estado["horarios"]
            ],
            value=str(grupo.get("id_horario")),
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar_edicion(e):
            if (
                not deporte_field.value
                or not nivel_field.value
                or not horario_field.value
            ):
                error_text.value = "Disciplina, nivel y horario son obligatorios."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.update_grupo_deporte(
                    grupo["id_grupo"],
                    {
                        "id_deporte": int(deporte_field.value),
                        "id_nivel": int(nivel_field.value),
                        "id_horario": int(horario_field.value),
                    },
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al actualizar el grupo deportivo."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Editar Grupo Deportivo #{grupo['id_grupo']}",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[deporte_field, nivel_field, horario_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Guardar Cambios",
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                    on_click=guardar_edicion,
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    def confirmar_eliminar_grupo(grupo: dict):
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def eliminar(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_grupo_deporte(grupo["id_grupo"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo eliminar el grupo deportivo."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                "Eliminar Grupo Deportivo", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"¿Confirma eliminar el grupo de «{grupo.get('deporte', '')}» "
                        f"({grupo.get('nivel', '-')} · {grupo.get('horario', '-')})? "
                        "Solo es posible si no tiene alumnos inscriptos.",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Eliminar", bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=eliminar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    aviso_info = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=14,
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.INFO_OUTLINE, size=18, color=TEAL),
                ft.Text(
                    "Cada grupo deportivo combina una disciplina, un nivel educativo y un horario. La cantidad de inscriptos se calcula con las inscripciones registradas.",
                    size=13,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    render()

    # ==================== 7. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Programa Deportivo, Grupos e Inscripciones",
        active_route="/admin/deportes",
        content_controls=[
            aviso_info,
            aviso_error,
            kpis_holder,
            filters_bar,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_horarios.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


async def get_admin_horarios_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista de Turnos y Horarios conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    # ==================== ESTADO DE LA VISTA ====================
    estado: dict = {"horarios": [], "texto": ""}

    try:
        estado["horarios"] = await api_client.get_horarios()
    except Exception:
        estado["horarios"] = []

    # ==================== 1. BARRA DE FILTROS Y ACCIÓN ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar horario o turno...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    btn_nuevo_horario = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Horario", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[search_input],
            ),
            btn_nuevo_horario,
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 2. TABLA DE HORARIOS ====================
    columnas_horarios = [
        ("Código", 140, False),
        ("Descripción del Horario", None, False),
        ("Acciones", 120, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_horarios)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def horario_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=140,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            f"HOR-{item['id_horario']}",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("descripcion", "-"),
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=6,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.EDIT_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=17,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Editar horario",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, h=item: abrir_editar_horario(h),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=17,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                    tooltip="Eliminar horario",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, h=item: (
                                        confirmar_eliminar_horario(h)
                                    ),
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def horarios_filtrados() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").strip().lower()
        resultado = []
        for h in estado["horarios"]:
            etiqueta = (
                f"{h.get('descripcion', '')} hor-{h.get('id_horario', '')}".lower()
            )
            if texto and texto not in etiqueta:
                continue
            resultado.append(h)
        return resultado

    def render():
        visibles = horarios_filtrados()
        if not estado["horarios"]:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay horarios cargados."
        else:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay horarios que coincidan con la búsqueda."

        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[horario_row(h) for h in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(mensaje_vacio, size=13, color=MUTED),
                )
            ],
            min_width=720,
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['horarios'])} horarios registrados",
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["horarios"] = await api_client.get_horarios()
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de horarios: {err}")
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar

    # ==================== 3. DIÁLOGO NUEVO HORARIO ====================
    def abrir_nuevo_horario(e):
        descripcion_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Descripción del horario *",
            hint_text="Ej: Turno Mañana (08:00 - 12:00)",
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            descripcion = (descripcion_field.value or "").strip()
            if not descripcion:
                error_text.value = "La descripción del horario es obligatoria."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.create_horario({"descripcion": descripcion})
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear el horario."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Nuevo Horario", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[descripcion_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Horario", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nuevo_horario.on_click = abrir_nuevo_horario

    # ==================== 3b. DIÁLOGOS EDITAR / ELIMINAR HORARIO ====================
    def abrir_editar_horario(horario: dict):
        descripcion_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Descripción del horario *",
            value=horario.get("descripcion") or "",
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar_edicion(e):
            descripcion = (descripcion_field.value or "").strip()
            if not descripcion:
                error_text.value = "La descripción del horario es obligatoria."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.update_horario(
                    horario["id_horario"], {"descripcion": descripcion}
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al actualizar el horario."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Editar Horario HOR-{horario['id_horario']}",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[descripcion_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Guardar Cambios",
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                    on_click=guardar_edicion,
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    def confirmar_eliminar_horario(horario: dict):
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def eliminar(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_horario(horario["id_horario"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo eliminar el horario."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Eliminar Horario", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"¿Confirma eliminar el horario «{horario.get('descripcion')}»? "
                        "Solo es posible si no está asignado a grupos o actividades.",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Eliminar", bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=eliminar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    render()

    # ==================== 4. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Gestión Académica — Turnos y Horarios",
        active_route="/admin/horarios",
        content_controls=[
            filters_bar,
            aviso_error,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_inscripciones.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    ONLINE_GREEN,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import status_badge
from frontend.app.components.table_view import create_table_header


def _fecha_legible(iso: str | None) -> str:
    """Convierte una fecha ISO (2026-10-18) al formato dd/mm/aaaa."""
    if not iso:
        return "—"
    partes = str(iso)[:10].split("-")
    if len(partes) == 3:
        return f"{partes[2]}/{partes[1]}/{partes[0]}"
    return str(iso)


def _nro_solicitud(solicitud: dict) -> str:
    anio = str(solicitud.get("fecha") or "")[:4] or "—"
    return f"#SOL-{anio}-{solicitud['id_solicitud']:04d}"


async def get_admin_inscripciones_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista de Solicitudes de Admisión conectada a la API (aprobación/rechazo reales)."""

    token = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token)

    solicitudes: list[dict] = []
    error_carga = None
    try:
        solicitudes = await api_client.get_solicitudes()
    except Exception:
        error_carga = "No se pudieron cargar las solicitudes desde el servidor."

    # ==================== 1. FILTROS Y ACCIÓN ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar postulante o DNI...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
        on_change=lambda _: render_tabla(),
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    dropdown_nivel = ft.Dropdown(
        hint_text="Nivel: Todos",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
        text_size=14,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
        options=[
            ft.dropdown.Option(opc)
            for opc in ["Todos", "Inicial", "Primario", "Secundario"]
        ],
    )
    dropdown_estado = ft.Dropdown(
        hint_text="Estado: Todos",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
        text_size=14,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
        options=[
            ft.dropdown.Option(opc)
            for opc in ["Todos", "Pendiente", "Aprobado", "Rechazado"]
        ],
    )
    dropdown_nivel.on_change = lambda _: render_tabla()
    dropdown_estado.on_change = lambda _: render_tabla()

    def filtro_dropdown(dropdown: ft.Dropdown, width: int) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            width=width,
            height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            content=dropdown,
        )

    async def exportar_listado(e):
        page.launch_url(api_client.reporte_url("solicitudes"))

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    search_input,
                    filtro_dropdown(dropdown_nivel, 140),
                    filtro_dropdown(dropdown_estado, 140),
                ],
            ),
            ft.Button(
                content=ft.Row(
                    spacing=8,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Icon(
                            ft.Icons.FILE_DOWNLOAD_OUTLINED, size=16, color=BG_WHITE
                        ),
                        ft.Text(
                            "Exportar Listado",
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=BG_WHITE,
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
                bgcolor=TEAL,
                height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
                style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                    shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
                    padding=ft.Padding(20, 10, 20, 10),
                ),
                on_click=exportar_listado,
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 2. TABLA DE SOLICITUDES ====================
    columnas_solicitudes = [
        ("N° Solicitud", 130, False),
        ("Aspirante / Postulante", 180, False),
        ("DNI", 120, False),
        ("Nivel Postulado", 180, False),
        ("Padre / Tutor", 160, False),
        ("Teléfono", 130, False),
        ("Fecha", 110, False),
        ("Estado", 110, False),
        ("Acciones de Secretaría", None, True),
    ]
    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_solicitudes)
    filas_column = ft.Column(spacing=0, controls=[header_table])

    async def cambiar_estado(e, solicitud: dict, nuevo_estado: str):
        try:
            await api_client.cambiar_estado_solicitud(
                solicitud["id_solicitud"], nuevo_estado
            )
        except Exception:
            page.show_dialog(
                ft.SnackBar(
                    ft.Text("No se pudo actualizar la solicitud.", color="white"),
                    bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                )
            )
            return
        solicitud["estado"] = nuevo_estado
        render_tabla()
        page.show_dialog(
            ft.SnackBar(
                ft.Text(
                    f"Solicitud #{solicitud['id_solicitud']} marcada como {nuevo_estado}.",
                    color="white",
                ),
                bgcolor=ONLINE_GREEN,
            )
        )

    def ver_documentacion(e, solicitud: dict):
        def dato(label: str, valor: str) -> ft.Row:
            return ft.Row(
                spacing=8,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"{label}:",
                        size=13,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                        color=MUTED,
                        width=150,
                    ),
                    ft.Text(valor or "—", size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR, expand=True),
                ],
            )

        dialog = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Legajo de Admisión {_nro_solicitud(solicitud)}",
                size=16,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                color=TEAL_DARK,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=10,
                width=420,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    dato("Postulante", solicitud.get("postulante")),
                    dato("DNI", solicitud.get("dni")),
                    dato(
                        "Fecha de nacimiento",
                        _fecha_legible(solicitud.get("fecha_nacimiento")),
                    ),
                    dato("Nivel postulado", solicitud.get("nivel")),
                    dato("Padre / Tutor", solicitud.get("tutor")),
                    dato("Teléfono", solicitud.get("telefono")),
                    dato("Correo", solicitud.get("correo")),
                    dato("Fecha de solicitud", _fecha_legible(solicitud.get("fecha"))),
                    dato("Estado", solicitud.get("estado")),
                    dato("Observaciones", solicitud.get("observaciones")),
                ],
            ),
            actions=[ft.TextButton("Cerrar", on_click=lambda _: page.pop_dialog())],
            actions_alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
        )
        page.show_dialog(dialog)

    def solicitud_row(solicitud: dict) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=130,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _nro_solicitud(solicitud),
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=180,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            solicitud.get("postulante") or "—",
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            solicitud.get("dni") or "—", size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=180,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            solicitud.get("nivel") or "—", size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=160,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            solicitud.get("tutor") or "—", size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=130,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            solicitud.get("telefono") or solicitud.get("correo") or "—",
                            size=13,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _fecha_legible(solicitud.get("fecha")), size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=status_badge(solicitud.get("estado") or "Pendiente"),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=12,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.CHECK_CIRCLE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=18,
                                    icon_color=ONLINE_GREEN,
                                    tooltip="Aprobar Admisión",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, s=solicitud: cambiar_estado(
                                        e, s, "Aprobado"
                                    ),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.REMOVE_RED_EYE_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=18,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Ver Documentación",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, s=solicitud: ver_documentacion(
                                        e, s
                                    ),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.CANCEL_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=18,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                    tooltip="Rechazar",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, s=solicitud: cambiar_estado(
                                        e, s, "Rechazado"
                                    ),
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    info_text = ft.Text("", size=14, color=MUTED)

    def render_tabla():
        texto = (search_field.value or "").strip().lower()
        nivel = dropdown_nivel.value
        estado = dropdown_estado.value

        filas = []
        for s in solicitudes:
            if (
                texto
                and texto not in (s.get("postulante") or "").lower()
                and texto not in (s.get("dni") or "")
            ):
                continue
            if nivel and nivel != "Todos" and nivel not in (s.get("nivel") or ""):
                continue
            if estado and estado != "Todos" and s.get("estado") != estado:
                continue
            filas.append(solicitud_row(s))

        filas_column.controls = [header_table, *filas]
        info_text.value = (
            f"Mostrando {len(filas)} de {len(solicitudes)} solicitudes recibidas"
        )
        page.update()

    render_tabla()

    if error_carga:
        aviso = ft.Container(
            bgcolor="#FDE8E8",
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            padding=12,
            content=ft.Text(error_carga, size=13, color=BADGE_RED_TXT),
        )
    else:
        aviso = ft.Container()

    tabla_card = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        clip_behavior=ft.ClipBehavior.ANTI_ALIAS,
        content=ft.Row(
            scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
            spacing=0,
            controls=[
                ft.Container(
                    width=1250,
                    content=filas_column,
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    pagination_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[info_text],
    )

    # ==================== 3. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Admisiones — Solicitudes de Inscripción",
        active_route="/admin/inscripciones",
        content_controls=[
            aviso,
            filters_bar,
            tabla_card,
            pagination_bar,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_materias.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


async def get_admin_materias_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista del catálogo de Materias y Asignaturas conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    # ==================== ESTADO DE LA VISTA ====================
    estado: dict = {
        "materias": [],
        "asignaciones": [],
        "niveles": [],
        "cursos": [],
        "profesores": [],
        "texto": "",
        "nivel": "Todos",
    }

    try:
        estado["materias"] = await api_client.get_materias()
    except Exception:
        estado["materias"] = []
    try:
        estado["asignaciones"] = await api_client.get_asignaciones()
    except Exception:
        estado["asignaciones"] = []
    try:
        estado["niveles"] = await api_client.get_niveles()
    except Exception:
        estado["niveles"] = []
    try:
        estado["cursos"] = await api_client.get_cursos()
    except Exception:
        estado["cursos"] = []
    try:
        estado["profesores"] = await api_client.get_profesores(solo_activos=False)
    except Exception:
        estado["profesores"] = []

    nombre_nivel = {n["id_nivel"]: n["nombre"] for n in estado["niveles"]}

    # Niveles reales presentes en las asignaciones (para el filtro)
    niveles_disponibles: list[str] = []
    for a in estado["asignaciones"]:
        nivel = a.get("nivel") or nombre_nivel.get(a.get("id_nivel"))
        if nivel and nivel not in niveles_disponibles:
            niveles_disponibles.append(nivel)

    # ==================== 1. FILTROS Y ACCIÓN ====================
