    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar materia...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    filtro_nivel_container = ft.Container(
        width=180,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content=ft.Dropdown(
            hint_text="Nivel: Todos",
            hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
            text_size=14,
            border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
            content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
            options=[ft.dropdown.Option("Todos")]
            + [ft.dropdown.Option(nivel) for nivel in niveles_disponibles],
        ),
    )
    filtro_nivel_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_nivel_container.content

    btn_nueva_materia = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nueva Materia", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    btn_asignar_materia = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.LINK, size=16, color=TEAL),
                ft.Text(
                    "Asignar Materia", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=TEAL
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(20, 10, 20, 10),
            side=ft.BorderSide(1, TEAL),
        ),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[search_input, filtro_nivel_container],
            ),
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[btn_asignar_materia, btn_nueva_materia],
            ),
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 2. TABLA DE MATERIAS ====================
    columnas_materias = [
        ("Código", 110, False),
        ("Asignatura / Materia", 220, False),
        ("Nivel Educativo", 200, False),
        ("Cursos que la Dictan", 280, False),
        ("Docente(s) Titulares", 280, False),
        ("Acciones", None, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_materias)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def fila_acciones(al_editar, al_eliminar) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            expand=True,
            alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=6,
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                controls=[
                    ft.IconButton(
                        icon=ft.Icons.EDIT_OUTLINED,
                        icon_size=17,
                        icon_color=TEAL,
                        tooltip="Editar materia",
                        padding=0,
                        on_click=al_editar,
                    ),
                    ft.IconButton(
                        icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                        icon_size=17,
                        icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                        tooltip="Eliminar materia",
                        padding=0,
                        on_click=al_eliminar,
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def niveles_materia(id_materia: int) -> list[str]:
        """Niveles educativos únicos de las asignaciones de una materia."""
        nombres: list[str] = []
        for a in estado["asignaciones"]:
            if a.get("id_materia") != id_materia:
                continue
            nivel = a.get("nivel") or nombre_nivel.get(a.get("id_nivel"))
            if nivel and nivel not in nombres:
                nombres.append(nivel)
        return nombres

    def datos_materia(id_materia: int) -> dict:
        """Agrupa cursos y docentes únicos de las asignaciones de una materia."""
        cursos: list[str] = []
        docentes: list[str] = []
        for a in estado["asignaciones"]:
            if a.get("id_materia") != id_materia:
                continue
            curso = a.get("curso")
            if curso and curso not in cursos:
                cursos.append(curso)
            docente = a.get("profesor")
            if docente and docente not in docentes:
                docentes.append(docente)

        niveles = niveles_materia(id_materia)
        return {
            "niveles": ", ".join(niveles) if niveles else "-",
            "cursos": ", ".join(cursos) if cursos else "-",
            "docentes": ", ".join(docentes) if docentes else "-",
        }

    def materia_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        datos = datos_materia(item["id_materia"])

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            f"MAT-{item['id_materia']}",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=220,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item["nombre"],
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=200,
                        content=ft.Text(datos["niveles"], size=14, color=MUTED),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=280,
                        content=ft.Text(datos["cursos"], size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=280,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            datos["docentes"],
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    fila_acciones(
                        lambda e, m=item: abrir_editar_materia(m),
                        lambda e, m=item: confirmar_eliminar_materia(m),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def materias_filtradas() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").strip().lower()
        resultado = []
        for m in estado["materias"]:
            if estado["nivel"] != "Todos" and estado["nivel"] not in niveles_materia(
                m["id_materia"]
            ):
                continue
            etiqueta = f"{m.get('nombre', '')} mat-{m.get('id_materia', '')}".lower()
            if texto and texto not in etiqueta:
                continue
            resultado.append(m)
        return resultado

    def render():
        visibles = materias_filtradas()
        if not estado["materias"]:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay materias cargadas."
        else:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay materias que coincidan con los filtros."

        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[materia_row(m) for m in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(mensaje_vacio, size=13, color=MUTED),
                )
            ],
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['materias'])} materias registradas",
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["materias"] = await api_client.get_materias()
            estado["asignaciones"] = await api_client.get_asignaciones()
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de materias: {err}")
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    def on_filtrar_nivel(e):
        estado["nivel"] = filtro_nivel_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar
    filtro_nivel_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_nivel

    # ==================== 3. DIÁLOGO NUEVA MATERIA ====================
    def abrir_nueva_materia(e):
        nombre_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nombre de la materia *",
            hint_text="Ej: Matemática",
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            nombre = (nombre_field.value or "").strip()
            if not nombre:
                error_text.value = "El nombre de la materia es obligatorio."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.create_materia({"nombre": nombre})
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear la materia."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Nueva Materia", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14, width=380, tight=True, controls=[nombre_field, error_text]
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Materia", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nueva_materia.on_click = abrir_nueva_materia

    # ==================== 4. DIÁLOGO ASIGNAR MATERIA ====================
    def abrir_asignar_materia(e):
        faltantes = []
        if not estado["materias"]:
            faltantes.append("materias")
        if not estado["cursos"]:
            faltantes.append("cursos")
        if not estado["profesores"]:
            faltantes.append("profesores")
        if faltantes:
            mostrar_error(
                f"No se puede asignar: no hay {' ni '.join(faltantes)} cargados."
            )
            return

        materia_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Materia *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(m["id_materia"]), text=m["nombre"])
                for m in estado["materias"]
            ],
        )
        curso_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Curso *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(key=str(c["id_curso"]), text=c["nombre"])
                for c in estado["cursos"]
            ],
        )
        profesor_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Profesor *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(
                    key=str(p["legajo"]),
                    text=f"{p['apellido']}, {p['nombre']} (#{p['legajo']})",
                )
                for p in estado["profesores"]
            ],
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            if not (materia_field.value and curso_field.value and profesor_field.value):
                error_text.value = "Debe seleccionar materia, curso y profesor."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.create_asignacion(
                    {
                        "id_materia": int(materia_field.value),
                        "id_curso": int(curso_field.value),
                        "legajo_profesor": int(profesor_field.value),
                    }
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear la asignación."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                "Asignar Materia a Curso", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "Seleccione la materia, el curso y el docente que la dictará.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    materia_field,
                    curso_field,
                    profesor_field,
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button("Asignar", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_asignar_materia.on_click = abrir_asignar_materia

    # ==================== 4b. DIÁLOGOS EDITAR / ELIMINAR MATERIA ====================
    def abrir_editar_materia(materia: dict):
        nombre_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nombre de la materia *",
            value=materia.get("nombre") or "",
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar_edicion(e):
            nombre = (nombre_field.value or "").strip()
            if not nombre:
                error_text.value = "El nombre de la materia es obligatorio."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.update_materia(
                    materia["id_materia"], {"nombre": nombre}
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al actualizar la materia."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Editar Materia MAT-{materia['id_materia']}",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14, width=380, tight=True, controls=[nombre_field, error_text]
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Guardar Cambios",
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                    on_click=guardar_edicion,
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    def confirmar_eliminar_materia(materia: dict):
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def eliminar(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_materia(materia["id_materia"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo eliminar la materia."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Eliminar Materia", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"¿Confirma eliminar la materia «{materia.get('nombre')}»? "
                        "Solo es posible si no tiene asignaciones ni calificaciones asociadas.",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Eliminar", bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=eliminar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    render()

    # ==================== 5. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Plan de Estudios — Materias y Asignaturas",
        active_route="/admin/materias",
        content_controls=[
            filters_bar,
            aviso_error,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_niveles.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    ORANGE,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import status_badge
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)

ICONOS_NIVEL = {
    "inicial": ft.Icons.CHILD_CARE,
    "primario": ft.Icons.MENU_BOOK_OUTLINED,
    "secundario": ft.Icons.SCHOOL_OUTLINED,
}


async def get_admin_niveles_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista de configuración y control de Niveles Educativos conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    estado: dict = {"niveles": [], "cursos": [], "alumnos": []}

    try:
        estado["niveles"] = await api_client.get_niveles()
    except Exception:
        estado["niveles"] = []
    try:
        estado["cursos"] = await api_client.get_cursos()
    except Exception:
        estado["cursos"] = []
    try:
        estado["alumnos"] = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=False)
    except Exception:
        estado["alumnos"] = []

    def cursos_del_nivel(id_nivel: int) -> list[dict]:
        return [c for c in estado["cursos"] if c.get("id_nivel") == id_nivel]

    def matricula_del_nivel(id_nivel: int) -> int:
        ids_cursos = {c["id_curso"] for c in cursos_del_nivel(id_nivel)}
        return len(
            [
                a
                for a in estado["alumnos"]
                if a.get("id_curso") in ids_cursos
                and a.get("estado_alumno") == "ACTIVO"
            ]
        )

    # ==================== 1. TARJETAS DE NIVELES (KPIS RESUMEN) ====================
    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    def nivel_kpi_card(nivel: dict) -> ft.Container:
        nombre = nivel.get("nombre", "")
        icono = ICONOS_NIVEL.get(nombre.strip().lower(), ft.Icons.SCHOOL_OUTLINED)
        cursos_count = len(cursos_del_nivel(nivel["id_nivel"]))
        matricula = matricula_del_nivel(nivel["id_nivel"])

        return ft.Container(
            expand=True,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
            padding=24,
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                controls=[
                    ft.Row(
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Container(
                                width=42,
                                height=42,
                                bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                                border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                content=ft.Icon(icono, size=22, color=TEAL_DARK),
                            ),
                            status_badge("Activo"),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=2,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                nombre,
                                size=18,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                f"Código interno: {nivel['id_nivel']}",
                                size=12,
                                color=ORANGE,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1, thickness=1),
                    ft.Row(
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text("Cursos Activos:", size=12, color=MUTED),
                            ft.Text(
                                str(cursos_count),
                                size=12,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEXT_COLOR,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Row(
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text("Matrícula Activa:", size=12, color=MUTED),
                            ft.Text(
                                str(matricula),
                                size=12,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEXT_COLOR,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def render_cards() -> ft.Row:
        return ft.Row(
            spacing=20,
            controls=[nivel_kpi_card(n) for n in estado["niveles"]]
            or [
                ft.Text(
                    "No hay niveles educativos cargados todavía.",
                    size=13,
                    color=MUTED,
                )
            ],
        )

    tabla_niveles_holder = ft.Container()
    cards_holder = ft.Container()

    # ==================== 2. DETALLE DE NIVELES ====================
    columnas_niveles = [
        ("Código", 110, False),
        ("Nivel Institucional", 240, False),
        ("Cursos Activos", 160, False),
        ("Matrícula Activa", 160, False),
        ("Estado", 100, False),
        ("Acciones", None, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_niveles)

    def fila_acciones(al_editar, al_eliminar) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            expand=True,
            alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=6,
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                controls=[
                    ft.IconButton(
                        icon=ft.Icons.EDIT_OUTLINED,
                        icon_size=17,
                        icon_color=TEAL,
                        tooltip="Editar nivel",
                        padding=0,
                        on_click=al_editar,
                    ),
                    ft.IconButton(
                        icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                        icon_size=17,
                        icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                        tooltip="Eliminar nivel",
                        padding=0,
                        on_click=al_eliminar,
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def nivel_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            f"NIV-{item['id_nivel']}",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=240,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item["nombre"],
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=160,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(len(cursos_del_nivel(item["id_nivel"]))),
                            size=13,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=160,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(matricula_del_nivel(item["id_nivel"])),
                            size=13,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(width=100, content=status_badge("Activo")),
                    fila_acciones(
                        lambda e, n=item: abrir_editar_nivel(n),
                        lambda e, n=item: confirmar_eliminar_nivel(n),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def render():
        cards_holder.content = render_cards()
        tabla_niveles_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[nivel_row(n) for n in estado["niveles"]]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "No hay niveles educativos cargados.", size=13, color=MUTED
                    ),
                )
            ],
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["niveles"] = await api_client.get_niveles()
            estado["cursos"] = await api_client.get_cursos()
            estado["alumnos"] = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=False)
        except Exception as err:
            aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ No se pudo refrescar la información: {err}"
            aviso_error.visible = True
        render()

    # ==================== 3. DIÁLOGO NUEVO NIVEL ====================
    btn_nuevo_nivel = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Nivel", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    def abrir_nuevo_nivel(e):
        nombre_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nombre del nivel *", hint_text="Ej: Terciario", width=380
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            nombre = (nombre_field.value or "").strip()
            if not nombre:
                error_text.value = "El nombre es obligatorio."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.create_nivel({"nombre": nombre})
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear el nivel."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Nuevo Nivel Educativo", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14, width=380, tight=True, controls=[nombre_field, error_text]
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Nivel", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nuevo_nivel.on_click = abrir_nuevo_nivel

    # ==================== 3b. DIÁLOGOS EDITAR / ELIMINAR NIVEL ====================
    def abrir_editar_nivel(nivel: dict):
        nombre_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nombre del nivel *",
            value=nivel.get("nombre") or "",
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar_edicion(e):
            nombre = (nombre_field.value or "").strip()
            if not nombre:
                error_text.value = "El nombre es obligatorio."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.update_nivel(nivel["id_nivel"], {"nombre": nombre})
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al actualizar el nivel."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Editar Nivel NIV-{nivel['id_nivel']}",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14, width=380, tight=True, controls=[nombre_field, error_text]
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Guardar Cambios",
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                    on_click=guardar_edicion,
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    def confirmar_eliminar_nivel(nivel: dict):
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def eliminar(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_nivel(nivel["id_nivel"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo eliminar el nivel."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                "Eliminar Nivel Educativo", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"¿Confirma eliminar el nivel «{nivel.get('nombre')}»? "
                        "Solo es posible si no tiene cursos asociados.",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Eliminar", bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=eliminar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    render()

    # ==================== 4. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Estructura Académica — Niveles Educativos K-12",
        active_route="/admin/niveles",
        content_controls=[
            aviso_error,
            cards_holder,
            ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "Detalle Pedagógico y Servicios por Nivel",
                        size=18,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                        color=TEAL_DARK,
                    ),
                    btn_nuevo_nivel,
                ],
            ),
            tabla_niveles_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_profesores.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import status_badge
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


async def get_admin_profesores_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista de la Nómina del Personal Docente conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    estado: dict = {"profesores": [], "texto": ""}

    try:
        estado["profesores"] = await api_client.get_profesores(solo_activos=False)
    except Exception:
        estado["profesores"] = []

    # ==================== 1. BARRA DE BÚSQUEDA Y FILTROS ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar docente por especialidad o apellido...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    def filtro_dropdown(hint: str, width: int, opciones: list[str]) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            width=width,
            height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            content=ft.Dropdown(
                hint_text=hint,
                hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                text_size=14,
                border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
                content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
                options=[ft.dropdown.Option(opc) for opc in opciones],
            ),
        )

    btn_nuevo_profesor = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Profesor",
                    size=14,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
        on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/profesores/nuevo"),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    search_input,
                    filtro_dropdown(
                        "Estado: Todos",
                        144,
                        ["Todos", "Activo", "Licencia", "Inactivo"],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
            btn_nuevo_profesor,
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 2. TABLA DE PROFESORES ====================
    columnas_profesores = [
        ("Legajo", 80, False),
        ("DNI", 105, False),
        ("Apellido", 120, False),
        ("Nombre", 130, False),
        ("Especialidad Principal", 180, False),
        ("Correo Electrónico", 190, False),
        ("Teléfono", 115, False),
        ("Estado", 95, False),
        ("Acciones", 80, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_profesores)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def profesor_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        async def eliminar_profesor(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_profesor(int(item["legajo"]))
            except Exception as err:
                mostrar_error(
                    f"No se pudo dar de baja al docente {item['legajo']}: {err}"
                )
                return
            await recargar()

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=80,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(item.get("legajo", "")),
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=105,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(item.get("dni", "")), size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("apellido", ""),
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=130,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("nombre", ""), size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=180,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("especialidad", ""), size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=190,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("correo_electronico", ""), size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=115,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("telefono", ""), size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=95,
                        content=status_badge(item.get("estado", "ACTIVO").capitalize()),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=80,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=8,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                    tooltip="Dar de baja",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=eliminar_profesor,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def profesores_filtrados() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").lower()
        if not texto:
            return estado["profesores"]
        return [
            p
            for p in estado["profesores"]
            if texto
            in f"{p.get('apellido', '')} {p.get('nombre', '')} {p.get('especialidad', '')} {p.get('legajo', '')}".lower()
        ]

    def render():
        visibles = profesores_filtrados()
        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[profesor_row(p) for p in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "No hay docentes que coincidan con la búsqueda.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                )
            ],
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['profesores'])} docentes registrados",
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["profesores"] = await api_client.get_profesores(solo_activos=False)
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de docentes: {err}")
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar

    render()

    # ==================== 3. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Nómina del Personal Docente",
        active_route="/admin/profesores",
        content_controls=[
            filters_bar,
            aviso_error,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_profesor_form.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_PAGE,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import dropdown_con_label, input_con_label

# El backend (y la BD) espera los estados en mayúsculas
ESTADO_API = {"Activo": "ACTIVO", "Licencia": "LICENCIA", "Inactivo": "INACTIVO"}


def _valor(campo: ft.Column) -> str:
    """Extrae el valor del control interno de input_con_label / dropdown_con_label."""
    return (campo.controls[1].content.value or "").strip()


async def get_admin_profesor_form_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista del formulario de alta de docentes conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    feedback_text = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== CAMPOS DEL FORMULARIO ====================
    campo_legajo = input_con_label("Legajo *", "Ej: 2005")
    campo_dni = input_con_label("DNI *", "Ej: 32.400.912")
    campo_nombres = input_con_label("Nombres *", "Ej: Guillermina")
    campo_apellidos = input_con_label("Apellidos *", "Ej: Sánchez")
    campo_especialidad = input_con_label(
        "Especialidad Principal *", "Ej: Matemática Aplicada, Álgebra"
    )
    campo_estado = dropdown_con_label(
        "Estado de Contrato *", "Activo", ["Activo", "Licencia", "Inactivo"]
    )
    campo_correo = input_con_label("Correo Laboral *", "ejemplo@educar.edu.ar")
    campo_telefono = input_con_label("Teléfono Particular *", "3624-XXXXXX")

    # ==================== 1. ENCABEZADO DE ACCIONES ====================
    btn_guardar = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Text(
            "Guardar Ficha", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=37,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(20, 10, 20, 10),
        ),
    )

    async def guardar_profesor(e):
        feedback_text.visible = False
        page.update()

        legajo = _valor(campo_legajo)
        dni = _valor(campo_dni)
        nombres = _valor(campo_nombres)
        apellidos = _valor(campo_apellidos)
        especialidad = _valor(campo_especialidad)
        estado = (campo_estado.controls[1].content.value or "Activo").strip()
        correo = _valor(campo_correo)
        telefono = _valor(campo_telefono)

        if not all([legajo, dni, nombres, apellidos, especialidad, correo, telefono]):
            feedback_text.value = "⚠️ Legajo, DNI, Nombres, Apellidos, Especialidad, Correo y Teléfono son obligatorios."
            feedback_text.color = BADGE_RED_TXT
            feedback_text.visible = True
            page.update()
            return

        try:
            legajo_int = int(legajo)
        except ValueError:
            feedback_text.value = "⚠️ El legajo debe ser un número entero."
            feedback_text.color = BADGE_RED_TXT
            feedback_text.visible = True
            page.update()
            return

        payload = {
            "legajo": legajo_int,
            "dni": dni,
            "nombre": nombres,
            "apellido": apellidos,
            "especialidad": especialidad,
            "correo_electronico": correo,
            "telefono": telefono,
            "estado": ESTADO_API.get(estado, "ACTIVO"),
        }

        btn_guardar.disabled = True
        btn_guardar.content = ft.Text(
            "Guardando...", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
        )
        page.update()

        try:
            await api_client.create_profesor(payload)
            page.go("/admin/profesores")
        except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
            btn_guardar.disabled = False
            btn_guardar.content = ft.Text(
                "Guardar Ficha", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
            )
            detalle = "Error al crear el docente."
            try:
                detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
            except Exception:
                pass
            feedback_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
            feedback_text.color = BADGE_RED_TXT
            feedback_text.visible = True
            page.update()
        except httpx.RequestError:
            btn_guardar.disabled = False
            btn_guardar.content = ft.Text(
                "Guardar Ficha", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
            )
            feedback_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
            feedback_text.color = BADGE_RED_TXT
            feedback_text.visible = True
            page.update()

    btn_guardar.on_click = guardar_profesor

    header_actions = ft.Column(
        spacing=8,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Row(
                        spacing=12,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Container(
                                width=36,
                                height=36,
                                bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                                border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                                border_radius=18,
                                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                tooltip="Volver a la nómina de profesores",
                                on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/profesores"),
                                content=ft.Icon(
                                    ft.Icons.ARROW_BACK, size=16, color=TEXT_COLOR
                                ),
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                "Nuevo Docente",
                                size=18,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Row(
                        spacing=12,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Button(
                                content=ft.Text(
                                    "Cancelar",
                                    size=14,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                    color=MUTED,
                                ),
                                bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                                height=37,
                                style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                                    side=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                                    shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                        radius=BORDER_RADIUS
                                    ),
                                    padding=ft.Padding(20, 10, 20, 10),
                                ),
                                on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/profesores"),
                            ),
                            btn_guardar,
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
            feedback_text,
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 2. COLUMNA IZQUIERDA FLUIDA ====================
    card_info_basica = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=20,
            controls=[
                ft.Text(
                    "Información Básica",
                    size=18,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                ),
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=16,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[campo_legajo, campo_dni],
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[campo_nombres, campo_apellidos],
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[campo_especialidad, campo_estado],
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=16,
                            controls=[campo_correo, campo_telefono],
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    columna_izquierda = ft.Column(
        expand=True,
        spacing=24,
        controls=[card_info_basica],
    )

    # ==================== 3. COLUMNA DERECHA COMPACTA ====================
    card_materias_cargo = ft.Container(
        width=380,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=20,
            controls=[
                ft.Column(
                    spacing=4,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Text(
                            "Materias a Cargo",
                            size=18,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                        ft.Text(
                            "Asigne las asignaturas correspondientes.",
                            size=12,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ],
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    bgcolor=BG_PAGE,
                    border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                    padding=16,
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "Guarde el docente y luego asigne materias y cursos desde el módulo "
                        '"Materias" (botón "Asignar Materia").',
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    # ==================== 4. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Ficha de Docente — Alta y Asignación Académica",
        active_route="/admin/profesores",
        content_controls=[
            header_actions,
            ft.Row(
                spacing=24,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.START,
                controls=[columna_izquierda, card_materias_cargo],
            ),
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_reportes.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_PAGE,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    MUTED,
    ORANGE,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)


def formatear_fecha(valor) -> str:
    """Convierte una fecha ISO (YYYY-MM-DD) al formato local DD/MM/YYYY."""
    texto = str(valor or "").strip()
    if not texto:
        return "—"
    partes = texto.split("-")
    if len(partes) == 3 and all(partes):
        return f"{partes[2]}/{partes[1]}/{partes[0]}"
    return texto


async def get_admin_reportes_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Índice general de reportes con indicadores reales y vista previa en vivo desde la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    estado: dict = {
        "alumnos": [],
        "profesores": [],
        "cursos": [],
        "inscripciones_deporte": [],
    }
    errores: list[str] = []

    async def consultar(etiqueta: str, metodo, *args, **kwargs) -> list:
        """Ejecuta una consulta a la API y acumula los fallos para avisar en pantalla."""
        try:
            return await metodo(*args, **kwargs)
        except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
            detalle = "error del servidor"
            try:
                detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
            except Exception:
                pass
            errores.append(f"{etiqueta}: {detalle}")
            return []
        except httpx.RequestError:
            errores.append(f"{etiqueta}: sin conexión con el backend")
            return []
        except Exception:
            errores.append(f"{etiqueta}: error inesperado")
            return []

    estado["alumnos"] = await consultar(
        "alumnos", api_client.get_alumnos, solo_activos=False
    )
    estado["profesores"] = await consultar(
        "docentes", api_client.get_profesores, solo_activos=False
    )
    estado["cursos"] = await consultar("cursos", api_client.get_cursos)
    estado["inscripciones_deporte"] = await consultar(
        "inscripciones deportivas", api_client.get_inscripciones_deporte
    )

    alumnos_activos = len(
        [
            a
            for a in estado["alumnos"]
            if str(a.get("estado_alumno", "")).upper() == "ACTIVO"
        ]
    )
    docentes_activos = len(
        [
            p
            for p in estado["profesores"]
            if str(p.get("estado", "")).upper() == "ACTIVO"
        ]
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )
    if errores:
        aviso_error.value = "⚠️ No se pudieron cargar algunos datos: " + " · ".join(
            errores
        )
        aviso_error.visible = True

    # ==================== 1. INDICADORES REALES ====================
    def kpi_card(
        titulo: str,
        valor: int,
        subtitulo: str,
        icono: ft.IconData,
        ruta: str,
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            col={"sm": 12, "md": 6, "lg": 3},
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=12,
            padding=18,
            on_click=lambda _: page.go(ruta),
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=14,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=40,
                        height=40,
                        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                        content=ft.Icon(icono, size=20, color=TEAL_DARK),
                    ),
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=2,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                str(valor),
                                size=22,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                titulo,
                                size=13,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                color=TEXT_COLOR,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                subtitulo,
                                size=11,
                                color=MUTED,
                                max_lines=1,
                                overflow=ft.TextOverflow.ELLIPSIS,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    kpis = ft.ResponsiveRow(
        columns=12,
        spacing=16,
        run_spacing=16,
        controls=[
            kpi_card(
                "Alumnos activos",
                alumnos_activos,
                f"{len(estado['alumnos'])} registrados en total",
                ft.Icons.PERSON_OUTLINE,
                "/admin/reporte-alumnos-docentes",
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "Docentes activos",
                docentes_activos,
                f"{len(estado['profesores'])} registrados en total",
                ft.Icons.WORK_OUTLINE,
                "/admin/reporte-alumnos-docentes",
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "Cursos y divisiones",
                len(estado["cursos"]),
                "Divisiones cargadas en el sistema",
                ft.Icons.CLASS_OUTLINED,
                "/admin/reporte-cursos-materias",
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "Inscriptos a deportes",
                len(estado["inscripciones_deporte"]),
                "Fichas de inscripción registradas",
                ft.Icons.FITNESS_CENTER_OUTLINED,
                "/admin/reporte-deportes-avanzado",
            ),
        ],
    )

    seccion_indicadores = ft.Column(
        spacing=16,
        controls=[
            ft.Text(
                "Indicadores Generales del Sistema",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                color=TEAL_DARK,
            ),
            kpis,
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 2. TARJETAS DE CATEGORÍAS RESPONSIVAS ====================
    def categoria_card(
        titulo: str, descripcion: str, icono: ft.IconData, ruta: str | None = None
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            col={"sm": 12, "md": 6, "lg": 4},
            height=116,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=12,
            padding=18,
            on_click=lambda _: page.go(ruta) if ruta else None,
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=10,
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                controls=[
                    ft.Row(
                        spacing=10,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Container(
                                width=32,
                                height=32,
                                bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                                border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                content=ft.Icon(icono, size=16, color=TEAL_DARK),
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                titulo,
                                size=15,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        descripcion,
                        size=12,
                        color=MUTED,
                        max_lines=2,
                        overflow=ft.TextOverflow.ELLIPSIS,
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    categorias_data = [
        (
            "Reporte por Alumno",
            "Nómina de alumnos por curso y nivel con su estado de matrícula.",
            ft.Icons.PERSON_OUTLINE,
            "/admin/reporte-alumnos-docentes",
        ),
        (
            "Reporte por Docente",
            "Plantel docente agrupado por especialidad y estado registrado.",
            ft.Icons.WORK_OUTLINE,
            "/admin/reporte-alumnos-docentes",
        ),
        (
            "Listado por Curso",
            "Cursos y divisiones con matrícula activa y materias asignadas.",
            ft.Icons.PEOPLE_OUTLINE,
            "/admin/reporte-cursos-materias",
        ),
        (
            "Listado por Materia",
            "Materias dictadas, docente a cargo e inscriptos por curso.",
            ft.Icons.MENU_BOOK_OUTLINED,
            "/admin/reporte-cursos-materias",
        ),
        (
            "Listado por Deporte",
            "Grupos, niveles, horarios e inscriptos por disciplina deportiva.",
            ft.Icons.FITNESS_CENTER_OUTLINED,
            "/admin/reporte-deportes-avanzado",
        ),
        (
            "Listado por Transporte",
            "Pasajeros por recorrido con estado de alta y datos de contacto.",
            ft.Icons.DIRECTIONS_BUS_OUTLINED,
            "/admin/reporte-transporte-pasajeros",
        ),
    ]

    seccion_categorias = ft.Column(
        spacing=16,
        controls=[
            ft.Text(
                "Categorías de Reportes Disponibles",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                color=TEAL_DARK,
            ),
            ft.ResponsiveRow(
                columns=12,
                spacing=16,
                run_spacing=16,
                controls=[categoria_card(t, d, i, r) for t, d, i, r in categorias_data],
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 3. VISTA PREVIA DE REPORTE (DATOS REALES) ====================
    nombre_curso = {c["id_curso"]: c["nombre"] for c in estado["cursos"]}
    curso_por_legajo = {a["legajo"]: a.get("id_curso") for a in estado["alumnos"]}

    ultimas_inscripciones = sorted(
        estado["inscripciones_deporte"],
        key=lambda i: str(i.get("fecha_inscripcion") or ""),
        reverse=True,
    )[:3]

    header_reporte = ft.Container(
        height=39,
        bgcolor=BG_PAGE,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Container(
                    width=120,
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "Legajo", size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEXT_COLOR
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    width=220,
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "Alumno", size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEXT_COLOR
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    width=150,
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "Curso", size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEXT_COLOR
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    width=180,
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "Disciplina Deportiva",
                        size=12,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                ),
                ft.Container(
                    expand=True,
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "Fecha de Inscripción",
                        size=12,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    def reporte_row(item: dict, con_borde: bool = True) -> ft.Container:
        curso = nombre_curso.get(
            curso_por_legajo.get(item.get("legajo_alumno")), "Sin curso asignado"
        )
        return ft.Container(
            height=40,
            padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR))
            if con_borde
            else None,
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            f"#{item.get('legajo_alumno', '')}",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=220,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(item.get("alumno", "")).split(" (")[0],
                            size=13,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=150, content=ft.Text(curso, size=13, color=TEXT_COLOR)
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=180,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("deporte") or "—",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            formatear_fecha(item.get("fecha_inscripcion")),
                            size=13,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    if ultimas_inscripciones:
        filas_preview = [
            reporte_row(item, con_borde=(indice < len(ultimas_inscripciones) - 1))
            for indice, item in enumerate(ultimas_inscripciones)
        ]
    else:
        filas_preview = [
            ft.Container(
                padding=20,
                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                content=ft.Text(
                    "No hay inscripciones deportivas registradas en el sistema.",
                    size=13,
                    color=MUTED,
                ),
            )
        ]

    tabla_preview = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=12,
        clip_behavior=ft.ClipBehavior.ANTI_ALIAS,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=0,
            controls=[
                header_reporte,
                *filas_preview,
            ],
        ),
    )

    preview_card = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
        padding=24,
        content=ft.Column(
            spacing=20,
            controls=[
                ft.Row(
                    alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                    vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                    controls=[
                        ft.Column(
                            spacing=4,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Text(
                                    "Vista Previa de Reporte: Inscripciones Deportivas",
                                    size=18,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                                ),
                                ft.Text(
                                    "Últimas fichas de inscripción registradas en las disciplinas deportivas.",
                                    size=13,
                                    color=MUTED,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                        ft.Row(
                            spacing=12,
                            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Button(
                                    content=ft.Row(
                                        spacing=8,
                                        controls=[
                                            ft.Icon(
                                                ft.Icons.FILE_DOWNLOAD_OUTLINED,
                                                size=14,
                                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                                            ),
                                            ft.Text(
                                                "Exportar Excel",
                                                size=13,
                                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                                            ),
                                        ],
                                    ),
                                    bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                                    height=32,
                                    style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                                        shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                            radius=BORDER_RADIUS
                                        ),
                                        padding=ft.Padding(16, 8, 16, 8),
                                    ),
                                    on_click=lambda _: page.launch_url(
                                        api_client.reporte_url("deportes")
                                    ),
                                ),
                                ft.Button(
                                    content=ft.Row(
                                        spacing=8,
                                        controls=[
                                            ft.Icon(
                                                ft.Icons.PRINT_OUTLINED,
                                                size=14,
                                                color=BG_WHITE,
                                            ),
                                            ft.Text(
                                                "Imprimir PDF",
                                                size=13,
                                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                                color=BG_WHITE,
