    pasajeros_inactivos = len(estado["inscripciones"]) - pasajeros_activos

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )
    if errores:
        aviso_error.value = "⚠️ No se pudieron cargar algunos datos: " + " · ".join(
            errores
        )
        aviso_error.visible = True

    # ==================== 1. ENCABEZADO Y ACCIONES ====================
    header_actions = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=36,
                        height=36,
                        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                        border_radius=18,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                        tooltip="Volver al Centro de Reportes",
                        on_click=lambda _: page.go("/admin/reportes"),
                        content=ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ARROW_BACK, size=16, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                    ),
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=2,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                "Hoja de Ruta: Pasajeros por Recorrido de Transporte",
                                size=18,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                "Planilla operativa con estado de alta y datos de contacto de cada pasajero",
                                size=12,
                                color=MUTED,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
            ft.Row(
                spacing=10,
                controls=[
                    ft.Button(
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=6,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Icon(
                                    ft.Icons.FILE_DOWNLOAD_OUTLINED,
                                    size=15,
                                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                                ),
                                ft.Text(
                                    "Exportar Excel",
                                    size=13,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                        height=36,
                        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS)
                        ),
                        on_click=lambda _: page.launch_url(
                            api_client.reporte_url("transporte")
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Button(
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=6,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Icon(
                                    ft.Icons.PRINT_OUTLINED, size=15, color=BG_WHITE
                                ),
                                ft.Text(
                                    "Imprimir PDF",
                                    size=13,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                    color=BG_WHITE,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                        bgcolor=ORANGE,
                        height=36,
                        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
                            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS)
                        ),
                        on_click=lambda _: None,
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 2. INDICADORES REALES ====================
    def kpi_card(
        titulo: str,
        valor: int,
        subtitulo: str,
        icono: ft.IconData,
        icono_color: str = TEAL_DARK,
        icono_bg: str = TEAL_LIGHT,
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            col={"sm": 12, "md": 6, "lg": 3},
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=12,
            padding=18,
            content=ft.Row(
                spacing=14,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=40,
                        height=40,
                        bgcolor=icono_bg,
                        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                        content=ft.Icon(icono, size=20, color=icono_color),
                    ),
                    ft.Column(
                        spacing=2,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text(
                                str(valor),
                                size=22,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                color=TEAL_DARK,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                titulo,
                                size=13,
                                weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                color=TEXT_COLOR,
                            ),
                            ft.Text(
                                subtitulo,
                                size=11,
                                color=MUTED,
                                max_lines=1,
                                overflow=ft.TextOverflow.ELLIPSIS,
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    kpis = ft.ResponsiveRow(
        columns=12,
        spacing=16,
        run_spacing=16,
        controls=[
            kpi_card(
                "Recorridos habilitados",
                len(estado["recorridos"]),
                "Rutas del servicio de transporte",
                ft.Icons.DIRECTIONS_BUS_OUTLINED,
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "Pasajeros registrados",
                len(estado["inscripciones"]),
                "Alumnos anotados en el servicio",
                ft.Icons.PEOPLE_OUTLINE,
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "Pasajeros activos",
                pasajeros_activos,
                "Altas vigentes en el servicio",
                ft.Icons.CHECK_CIRCLE_OUTLINE,
                ONLINE_GREEN,
                GREEN_LIGHT,
            ),
            kpi_card(
                "Pasajeros inactivos",
                pasajeros_inactivos,
                "Altas dadas de baja",
                ft.Icons.REMOVE_CIRCLE_OUTLINE,
                BADGE_RED_TXT,
                BADGE_RED_BG,
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 3. RESUMEN POR RECORRIDO ====================
    def recorrido_card(recorrido: dict) -> ft.Container:
        pasajeros = [
            i
            for i in estado["inscripciones"]
            if i.get("id_recorrido") == recorrido["id_recorrido"]
        ]
        total = len(pasajeros)
        activos = len(
            [p for p in pasajeros if str(p.get("estado", "")).upper() == "ACTIVO"]
        )
        ancho = int(220 * activos / total) if total else 0
        return ft.Container(
            col={"sm": 12, "md": 6},
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
            padding=18,
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=10,
                controls=[
                    ft.Row(
                        spacing=10,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Container(
                                width=32,
                                height=32,
                                bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                                border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
                                alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                content=ft.Icon(
                                    ft.Icons.DIRECTIONS_BUS_OUTLINED,
                                    size=16,
                                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                                ),
                            ),
                            ft.Container(
                                expand=True,
                                content=ft.Text(
                                    recorrido.get("descripcion")
                                    or f"Recorrido {recorrido.get('id_recorrido')}",
                                    size=15,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                                    max_lines=1,
                                    overflow=ft.TextOverflow.ELLIPSIS,
                                ),
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        f"{total} pasajeros registrados · {activos} activos",
                        size=12,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                        color=TEAL,
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=220,
                        height=10,
                        bgcolor=BG_PAGE,
                        border_radius=5,
                        clip_behavior=ft.ClipBehavior.ANTI_ALIAS,
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=0,
                            controls=[
                                ft.Container(
                                    width=max(ancho, 4) if activos else 0,
                                    height=10,
                                    bgcolor=ONLINE_GREEN,
                                    border_radius=5,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    seccion_resumen = ft.Column(
        spacing=16,
        controls=[
            ft.Text(
                "Resumen de Pasajeros por Recorrido",
                size=16,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                color=TEAL_DARK,
            ),
            ft.ResponsiveRow(
                columns=12,
                spacing=16,
                run_spacing=16,
                controls=[recorrido_card(r) for r in estado["recorridos"]]
                or [
                    ft.Container(
                        col={"sm": 12},
                        padding=20,
                        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                        border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                        content=ft.Text(
                            "No hay recorridos de transporte registrados en el sistema.",
                            size=13,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    )
                ],
            ),
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 4. FILTROS ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar alumno o legajo...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    def filtro_dropdown(hint: str, width: int, opciones: list[str]) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            width=width,
            height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            content=ft.Dropdown(
                hint_text=hint,
                hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                text_size=14,
                border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
                content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
                options=[ft.dropdown.Option(opc) for opc in opciones],
            ),
        )

    filtro_recorrido_container = filtro_dropdown(
        "Recorrido: Todos",
        230,
        ["Todos"]
        + [
            r.get("descripcion") or f"Recorrido {r.get('id_recorrido')}"
            for r in estado["recorridos"]
        ],
    )
    filtro_recorrido: ft.Dropdown = filtro_recorrido_container.content
    filtro_nivel_container = filtro_dropdown(
        "Nivel: Todos", 150, ["Todos"] + [n["nombre"] for n in estado["niveles"]]
    )
    filtro_nivel: ft.Dropdown = filtro_nivel_container.content
    filtro_pasajero_container = filtro_dropdown(
        "Estado: Todos", 145, ["Todos", "Activo", "Inactivo"]
    )
    filtro_pasajero: ft.Dropdown = filtro_pasajero_container.content

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        spacing=12,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            search_input,
            filtro_recorrido_container,
            filtro_nivel_container,
            filtro_pasajero_container,
        ],
    )

    # ==================== 5. TABLA DE PASAJEROS ====================
    columnas_transporte = [
        ("Recorrido Oficial", 160, False),
        ("Legajo", 90, False),
        ("Alumno Pasajero", 180, False),
        ("Curso", 120, False),
        ("Domicilio / Parada", 220, False),
        ("Teléfono Registrado", 140, False),
        ("Fecha de Alta", 120, False),
        ("Estado", 90, False),
    ]
    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_transporte)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def pasajero_row(inscripcion: dict) -> ft.Container:
        legajo = inscripcion.get("legajo_alumno")
        alumno = alumno_por_legajo.get(legajo) or {}
        return ft.Container(
            height=50,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=160,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            inscripcion.get("recorrido") or "—",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=90,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            f"#{legajo}", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=TEAL
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=180,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            str(inscripcion.get("alumno", "")).split(" (")[0],
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            curso_del_pasajero(legajo), size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=220,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            alumno.get("domicilio") or "Sin domicilio registrado",
                            size=13,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                            max_lines=1,
                            overflow=ft.TextOverflow.ELLIPSIS,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=140,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            alumno.get("telefono") or "Sin teléfono",
                            size=13,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=120,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            formatear_fecha(inscripcion.get("fecha_alta")),
                            size=12,
                            color=MUTED,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=90,
                        content=status_badge(inscripcion.get("estado") or "Sin estado"),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def pasajeros_filtrados() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").lower()
        resultado = []
        for inscripcion in estado["inscripciones"]:
            if (
                estado["recorrido"] != "Todos"
                and (inscripcion.get("recorrido") or "") != estado["recorrido"]
            ):
                continue
            if (
                estado["nivel"] != "Todos"
                and nivel_del_pasajero(inscripcion.get("legajo_alumno"))
                != estado["nivel"]
            ):
                continue
            if (
                estado["pasajero"] != "Todos"
                and str(inscripcion.get("estado") or "").capitalize()
                != estado["pasajero"]
            ):
                continue
            if (
                texto
                and texto
                not in f"{inscripcion.get('alumno', '')} {inscripcion.get('legajo_alumno', '')} {inscripcion.get('recorrido', '')}".lower()
            ):
                continue
            resultado.append(inscripcion)
        return resultado

    def render():
        visibles = pasajeros_filtrados()
        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[pasajero_row(p) for p in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "No hay pasajeros que coincidan con los filtros aplicados.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                )
            ],
            min_width=1152,
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['inscripciones'])} pasajeros registrados en el transporte escolar",
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    def on_filtrar_recorrido(e):
        estado["recorrido"] = filtro_recorrido.value or "Todos"
        render()

    def on_filtrar_nivel(e):
        estado["nivel"] = filtro_nivel.value or "Todos"
        render()

    def on_filtrar_pasajero(e):
        estado["pasajero"] = filtro_pasajero.value or "Todos"
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar
    filtro_recorrido.on_change = on_filtrar_recorrido
    filtro_nivel.on_change = on_filtrar_nivel
    filtro_pasajero.on_change = on_filtrar_pasajero

    render()

    # ==================== 6. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Reporte: Alumnos por Recorrido de Transporte",
        active_route="/admin/reportes",
        content_controls=[
            header_actions,
            aviso_error,
            kpis,
            seccion_resumen,
            filters_bar,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_transporte.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CARD_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import status_badge
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)


def _fecha_corta(iso: str | None) -> str:
    """Convierte un ISO timestamp del backend en dd/mm/aaaa."""
    if not iso or len(iso) < 10:
        return "-"
    return f"{iso[8:10]}/{iso[5:7]}/{iso[0:4]}"


def _nombre_alumno(valor: str) -> str:
    """Quita el sufijo '(#legajo)' para mostrar el nombre simple en la columna Estudiante."""
    texto = (valor or "").strip()
    if " (#" in texto:
        texto = texto.rsplit(" (#", 1)[0].strip()
    return texto or "-"


async def get_admin_transporte_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista del Servicio de Transporte Escolar conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    # ==================== 1. ESTADO DE LA VISTA ====================
    estado: dict = {
        "recorridos": [],
        "inscripciones": [],
        "texto": "",
        "recorrido": "Todos",
    }

    try:
        estado["recorridos"] = await api_client.get_recorridos()
    except Exception:
        estado["recorridos"] = []

    try:
        estado["inscripciones"] = await api_client.get_inscripciones_transporte()
    except Exception:
        estado["inscripciones"] = []

    def esta_activo(item: dict) -> bool:
        return (item.get("estado") or "").strip().upper() == "ACTIVO"

    def pasajeros_activos_de(id_recorrido) -> int:
        return len(
            [
                i
                for i in estado["inscripciones"]
                if i.get("id_recorrido") == id_recorrido and esta_activo(i)
            ]
        )

    # ==================== 2. TARJETAS DE RECORRIDOS (DATOS REALES) ====================
    def recorrido_card(num: int, recorrido: dict, activos: int) -> ft.Container:
        descripcion = recorrido.get("descripcion", "-")
        return ft.Container(
            width=280,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
            padding=20,
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=12,
                controls=[
                    ft.Row(
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Row(
                                spacing=8,
                                controls=[
                                    ft.Container(
                                        width=28,
                                        height=28,
                                        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                                        border_radius=14,
                                        alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                                        content=ft.Text(
                                            str(num),
                                            size=12,
                                            weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                                        ),
                                    ),
                                    ft.Text(
                                        f"Recorrido {num}",
                                        size=15,
                                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                        color=TEAL_DARK,
                                    ),
                                ],
                            ),
                            ft.Container(
                                padding=ft.Padding(8, 4, 8, 4),
                                bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
                                border_radius=4,
                                content=ft.Text(
                                    f"{activos} activos"
                                    if activos != 1
                                    else "1 activo",
                                    size=12,
                                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                                    color=TEAL,
                                ),
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                    ft.Text(
                        descripcion,
                        size=13,
                        weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    ft.Divider(color=BORDER_COLOR, height=1, thickness=1),
                    ft.Row(
                        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
                        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                        controls=[
                            ft.Text("Pasajeros activos", size=11, color=MUTED),
                            ft.Row(
                                spacing=2,
                                controls=[
                                    ft.Text(
                                        str(activos),
                                        size=11,
                                        weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
                                        color=TEAL_DARK,
                                    ),
                                    ft.IconButton(
                                        icon=ft.Icons.EDIT_OUTLINED,
                                        icon_size=15,
                                        icon_color=TEAL,
                                        tooltip="Editar recorrido",
                                        padding=0,
                                        on_click=lambda e, r=recorrido: (
                                            abrir_editar_recorrido(r)
                                        ),
                                    ),
                                    ft.IconButton(
                                        icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                        icon_size=15,
                                        icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                        tooltip="Eliminar recorrido",
                                        padding=0,
                                        on_click=lambda e, r=recorrido: (
                                            confirmar_eliminar_recorrido(r)
                                        ),
                                    ),
                                ],
                            ),
                        ],
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def construir_banner() -> ft.Control:
        if not estado["recorridos"]:
            return ft.Container(
                bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
                border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
                border_radius=CARD_RADIUS,
                padding=20,
                content=ft.Text(
                    "No hay recorridos cargados. Registre el primero con «Nuevo Recorrido».",
                    size=13,
                    color=MUTED,
                ),
            )
        return ft.Row(
            spacing=16,
            scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO,
            controls=[
                recorrido_card(indice, r, pasajeros_activos_de(r.get("id_recorrido")))
                for indice, r in enumerate(estado["recorridos"], start=1)
            ],
        )

    banner_holder = ft.Container()

    # ==================== 3. BARRA DE FILTROS Y ACCIONES ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar alumno o recorrido...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    filtro_recorrido_container = ft.Container(
        width=240,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        content=ft.Dropdown(
            hint_text="Recorrido: Todos",
            hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
            text_size=14,
            border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
            content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
            options=[ft.dropdown.Option("Todos")]
            + [
                ft.dropdown.Option(r.get("descripcion", "-"))
                for r in estado["recorridos"]
            ],
        ),
    )
    filtro_recorrido_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_recorrido_container.content

    btn_nuevo_recorrido = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.ADD_ROAD, size=16, color=TEAL),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Recorrido", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=TEAL
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(20, 12, 20, 12),
            side=ft.BorderSide(1, TEAL),
        ),
    )

    btn_inscribir = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.PERSON_ADD, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Inscribir Alumno",
                    size=14,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[search_input, filtro_recorrido_container],
            ),
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[btn_nuevo_recorrido, btn_inscribir],
            ),
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 4. TABLA DE PASAJEROS ====================
    columnas_transporte = [
        ("Legajo", 100, False),
        ("Estudiante", 240, False),
        ("Recorrido Asignado", 240, False),
        ("Fecha de Alta", 130, False),
        ("Estado", 110, False),
        ("Acciones", None, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_transporte)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def pasajero_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        activo = esta_activo(item)

        async def dar_de_baja(e):
            try:
                await api_client.baja_transporte(item["legajo_alumno"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo dar de baja la inscripción."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                mostrar_error(detalle)
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                mostrar_error("Error de conexión con el backend.")
                return
            await recargar()

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=100,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            f"#{item['legajo_alumno']}",
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=240,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _nombre_alumno(item.get("alumno", "")),
                            size=14,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEXT_COLOR,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=240,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("recorrido", "-"),
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                            color=TEAL,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=130,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _fecha_corta(item.get("fecha_alta")), size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=status_badge("Activo" if activo else "Inactivo"),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=12,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.DELETE_OUTLINE,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=BADGE_RED_TXT
                                    if activo
                                    else BORDER_COLOR,
                                    tooltip="Baja del Servicio"
                                    if activo
                                    else "Inscripción ya dada de baja",
                                    padding=0,
                                    disabled=not activo,
                                    on_click=dar_de_baja if activo else None,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def inscripciones_filtradas() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").lower()
        resultado = []
        for i in estado["inscripciones"]:
            if (
                estado["recorrido"] != "Todos"
                and i.get("recorrido") != estado["recorrido"]
            ):
                continue
            if (
                texto
                and texto
                not in f"{i.get('alumno', '')} {i.get('legajo_alumno', '')} {i.get('recorrido', '')}".lower()
            ):
                continue
            resultado.append(i)
        return resultado

    def render():
        banner_holder.content = construir_banner()
        visibles = inscripciones_filtradas()
        activos_totales = len([i for i in estado["inscripciones"] if esta_activo(i)])

        if not estado["inscripciones"]:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay alumnos inscriptos al transporte todavía."
        else:
            mensaje_vacio = "No hay pasajeros que coincidan con los filtros."

        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[pasajero_row(i) for i in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(mensaje_vacio, size=13, color=MUTED),
                )
            ],
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=(
                f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['inscripciones'])} inscripciones "
                f"· {activos_totales} pasajeros activos"
            ),
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def actualizar_filtro_recorridos():
        descripciones = [r.get("descripcion", "-") for r in estado["recorridos"]]
        filtro_recorrido_dropdown.options = [ft.dropdown.Option("Todos")] + [
            ft.dropdown.Option(d) for d in descripciones
        ]
        if estado["recorrido"] != "Todos" and estado["recorrido"] not in descripciones:
            estado["recorrido"] = "Todos"
            filtro_recorrido_dropdown.value = None

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["inscripciones"] = await api_client.get_inscripciones_transporte()
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de pasajeros: {err}")
        try:
            estado["recorridos"] = await api_client.get_recorridos()
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de recorridos: {err}")
        actualizar_filtro_recorridos()
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    def on_filtrar_recorrido(e):
        estado["recorrido"] = filtro_recorrido_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar
    filtro_recorrido_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_recorrido

    # ==================== 5. DIÁLOGO NUEVO RECORRIDO ====================
    def abrir_nuevo_recorrido(e):
        descripcion_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Descripción del recorrido *",
            hint_text="Ej: Ruta Norte - La Liguria",
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar(e):
            descripcion = (descripcion_field.value or "").strip()
            if not descripcion:
                error_text.value = "La descripción es obligatoria."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.create_recorrido({"descripcion": descripcion})
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear el recorrido."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Nuevo Recorrido", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "Registre la zona o ruta del servicio de transporte escolar.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    descripcion_field,
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Recorrido", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    # ==================== 5b. DIÁLOGOS EDITAR / ELIMINAR RECORRIDO ====================
    def abrir_editar_recorrido(recorrido: dict):
        descripcion_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Descripción del recorrido *",
            value=recorrido.get("descripcion") or "",
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def guardar_edicion(e):
            descripcion = (descripcion_field.value or "").strip()
            if not descripcion:
                error_text.value = "La descripción es obligatoria."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.update_recorrido(
                    recorrido["id_recorrido"], {"descripcion": descripcion}
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al actualizar el recorrido."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Editar Recorrido #{recorrido['id_recorrido']}",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[descripcion_field, error_text],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Guardar Cambios",
                    bgcolor=TEAL,
                    color=BG_WHITE,
                    on_click=guardar_edicion,
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    def confirmar_eliminar_recorrido(recorrido: dict):
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def eliminar(e):
            try:
                await api_client.delete_recorrido(recorrido["id_recorrido"])
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo eliminar el recorrido."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Eliminar Recorrido", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        f"¿Confirma eliminar el recorrido «{recorrido.get('descripcion')}»? "
                        "Solo es posible si no tiene pasajeros inscriptos.",
                        size=13,
                        color=TEXT_COLOR,
                    ),
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Eliminar", bgcolor=BADGE_RED_TXT, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=eliminar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    # ==================== 6. DIÁLOGO INSCRIBIR ALUMNO ====================
    async def abrir_inscribir_alumno(e):
        try:
            alumnos = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=True)
        except Exception:
            alumnos = []

        alumno_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Alumno *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(
                    key=str(a["legajo"]),
                    text=f"{a.get('apellido', '')}, {a.get('nombre', '')} (#{a['legajo']})",
                )
                for a in alumnos
            ],
        )
        recorrido_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Recorrido *",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(
                    key=str(r["id_recorrido"]), text=r.get("descripcion", "-")
                )
                for r in estado["recorridos"]
            ],
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        if not alumnos:
            error_text.value = "⚠️ No hay alumnos activos disponibles para inscribir."
            error_text.visible = True
        elif not estado["recorridos"]:
            error_text.value = (
                "⚠️ No hay recorridos cargados. Cree un recorrido primero."
            )
            error_text.visible = True

        async def guardar(e):
            if not alumno_field.value or not recorrido_field.value:
                error_text.value = "Alumno y recorrido son obligatorios."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.inscribir_transporte(
                    {
                        "legajo_alumno": int(alumno_field.value),
                        "id_recorrido": int(recorrido_field.value),
                    }
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al inscribir al alumno en el transporte."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                "Inscribir Alumno al Transporte", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "Seleccione el alumno y el recorrido que utilizará durante el ciclo lectivo.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    alumno_field,
                    recorrido_field,
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button("Inscribir", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nuevo_recorrido.on_click = abrir_nuevo_recorrido
    btn_inscribir.on_click = abrir_inscribir_alumno

    render()

    # ==================== 7. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Servicio de Transporte Escolar",
        active_route="/admin/transporte",
        content_controls=[
            banner_holder,
            filters_bar,
            aviso_error,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\admin_usuarios.py ---
======================================================================

import flet as ft
import httpx

from frontend.app.api_client import api_client
from frontend.app.components.admin_layout import admin_layout
from frontend.app.components.constants import (
    BADGE_RED_TXT,
    BG_WHITE,
    BORDER_COLOR,
    BORDER_RADIUS,
    CONTROL_HEIGHT,
    MUTED,
    ORANGE,
    TEAL,
    TEAL_DARK,
    TEAL_LIGHT,
    TEXT_COLOR,
)
from frontend.app.components.form_controls import status_badge
from frontend.app.components.table_view import (
    create_pagination_bar,
    create_table_container,
    create_table_header,
)

# Nombre legible para cada rol del backend
ROL_DISPLAY = {
    "ADMIN": "Administrador",
    "TEACHER": "Docente",
    "PARENT": "Padre / Tutor",
    "STUDENT": "Alumno",
}
ROL_API = {valor: clave for clave, valor in ROL_DISPLAY.items()}


def _fecha_corta(iso: str | None) -> str:
    """Convierte un ISO timestamp del backend en dd/mm/aaaa."""
    if not iso or len(iso) < 10:
        return "-"
    return f"{iso[8:10]}/{iso[5:7]}/{iso[0:4]}"


async def get_admin_usuarios_view(page: ft.Page) -> ft.Row:
    """Vista de Usuarios, Control de Acceso RBAC conectada a la API."""

    token_almacenado = getattr(page, "auth_token", None)
    if token_almacenado and not api_client.token:
        api_client.set_token(token_almacenado)

    # ==================== ESTADO DE LA VISTA ====================
    estado: dict = {
        "usuarios": [],
        "roles": [],
        "texto": "",
        "rol": "Todos",
        "activo": "Todos",
    }

    try:
        estado["usuarios"] = await api_client.get_usuarios()
    except Exception:
        estado["usuarios"] = []

    try:
        estado["roles"] = await api_client.get_roles()
    except Exception:
        estado["roles"] = []

    # ==================== 1. FILTROS Y ACCIÓN ====================
    search_field = ft.TextField(
        hint_text="Buscar usuario o vínculo...",
        hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=MUTED),
        text_size=14,
        color=TEXT_COLOR,
        border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
        content_padding=ft.Padding(0, 0, 0, 10),
        expand=True,
    )
    search_input = ft.Container(
        width=280,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
        border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 12, 0),
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.SEARCH, size=16, color=MUTED),
                search_field,
            ],
        ),
    )

    def crear_filtro_dropdown(
        hint: str, width: int, opciones: list[str]
    ) -> ft.Container:
        return ft.Container(
            width=width,
            height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
            bgcolor=BG_WHITE,
            border=ft.Border.all(1, BORDER_COLOR),
            border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
            content=ft.Dropdown(
                hint_text=hint,
                hint_style=ft.TextStyle(size=14, color=TEXT_COLOR),
                text_size=14,
                border=ft.InputBorder.NONE,
                content_padding=ft.Padding(12, 0, 8, 12),
                options=[ft.dropdown.Option(opc) for opc in opciones],
            ),
        )

    filtro_rol_container = crear_filtro_dropdown(
        "Rol: Todos",
        150,
        ["Todos", "Administrador", "Docente", "Padre / Tutor", "Alumno"],
    )
    filtro_estado_container = crear_filtro_dropdown(
        "Estado: Todos", 140, ["Todos", "Activo", "Inactivo"]
    )
    filtro_rol_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_rol_container.content
    filtro_estado_dropdown: ft.Dropdown = filtro_estado_container.content

    btn_nuevo_usuario = ft.Button(
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=8,
            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.PERSON_ADD_ALT_1_OUTLINED, size=16, color=BG_WHITE),
                ft.Text(
                    "Nuevo Usuario", size=14, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BG_WHITE
                ),
            ],
        ),
        bgcolor=TEAL,
        height=CONTROL_HEIGHT,
        style=ft.ButtonStyle(
            shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=BORDER_RADIUS),
            padding=ft.Padding(24, 12, 24, 12),
        ),
    )

    filters_bar = ft.Row(
        alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN,
        vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
        controls=[
            ft.Row(
                spacing=12,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[search_input, filtro_rol_container, filtro_estado_container],
            ),
            btn_nuevo_usuario,
        ],
    )

    aviso_error = ft.Text(
        "", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False
    )

    # ==================== 2. TABLA DE USUARIOS Y ROLES ====================
    columnas_usuarios = [
        ("Usuario", 200, False),
        ("Rol Asignado", 140, False),
        ("Vinculación / Perfil", 260, False),
        ("Alta", 110, False),
        ("Estado", 100, False),
        ("Acciones", None, True),
    ]

    header_table = create_table_header(columnas_usuarios)
    table_holder = ft.Container()
    pagination_holder = ft.Container()

    def rol_badge(rol: str) -> ft.Container:
        colores = {
            "Administrador": (TEAL_DARK, BG_WHITE),
            "Docente": (TEAL_LIGHT, TEAL_DARK),
            "Padre / Tutor": ("#FFF3E7", ORANGE),
            "Alumno": ("#EBFEE6", "#10B981"),
        }
        bg, color = colores.get(rol, (TEAL_LIGHT, TEAL_DARK))
        return ft.Container(
            padding=ft.Padding(8, 3, 8, 3),
            bgcolor=bg,
            border_radius=4,
            content=ft.Text(rol, size=11, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=color),
        )

    def mostrar_error(mensaje: str):
        aviso_error.value = f"⚠️ {mensaje}"
        aviso_error.visible = True
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def abrir_reset_password(usuario: dict):
        password_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Nueva contraseña",
            password=True,
            can_reveal_password=True,
            width=380,
        )
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        async def confirmar(e):
            nueva = (password_field.value or "").strip()
            if len(nueva) < 6:
                error_text.value = "La contraseña debe tener al menos 6 caracteres."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            try:
                await api_client.update_usuario(
                    usuario["id_usuario"], {"password": nueva}
                )
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "No se pudo actualizar la contraseña."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text(
                f"Restablecer contraseña — {usuario['username']}",
                size=18,
                weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD,
            ),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=12,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "La contraseña anterior dejará de funcionar.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    password_field,
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button("Guardar", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=confirmar),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    def usuario_row(item: dict) -> ft.Container:
        rol_legible = ROL_DISPLAY.get(item.get("rol", ""), item.get("rol", "-"))
        activo = bool(item.get("activo", True))

        async def toggle_estado(e):
            try:
                await api_client.update_usuario(
                    item["id_usuario"], {"activo": not activo}
                )
            except Exception as err:
                mostrar_error(
                    f"No se pudo cambiar el estado de '{item['username']}': {err}"
                )
                return
            await recargar()

        return ft.Container(
            height=54,
            padding=ft.Padding(16, 0, 16, 0),
            border=ft.Border(bottom=ft.BorderSide(1, BORDER_COLOR)),
            content=ft.Row(
                alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START,
                vertical_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER,
                controls=[
                    ft.Container(
                        width=200,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item["username"],
                            size=13,
                            weight=ft.FontWeight.W_600,
                            color=TEAL_DARK,
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(width=140, content=rol_badge(rol_legible)),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=260,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            item.get("vinculacion", "-"), size=13, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=110,
                        content=ft.Text(
                            _fecha_corta(item.get("creado_en")), size=12, color=MUTED
                        ),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        width=100,
                        content=status_badge("Activo" if activo else "Bloqueado"),
                    ),
                    ft.Container(
                        expand=True,
                        alignment=ft.Alignment(1, 0),
                        content=ft.Row(
                            spacing=12,
                            alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.END,
                            controls=[
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.KEY_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color=TEAL,
                                    tooltip="Restablecer Contraseña",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=lambda e, u=item: abrir_reset_password(u),
                                ),
                                ft.IconButton(
                                    icon=ft.Icons.LOCK_OPEN_OUTLINED
                                    if not activo
                                    else ft.Icons.BLOCK_OUTLINED,
                                    icon_size=16,
                                    icon_color="#10B981"
                                    if not activo
                                    else BADGE_RED_TXT,
                                    tooltip="Reactivar Acceso"
                                    if not activo
                                    else "Bloquear Acceso",
                                    padding=0,
                                    on_click=toggle_estado,
                                ),
                            ],
                        ),
                    ),
                ],
            ),
        )

    def usuarios_filtrados() -> list[dict]:
        texto = (estado["texto"] or "").lower()
        resultado = []
        for u in estado["usuarios"]:
            rol_legible = ROL_DISPLAY.get(u.get("rol", ""), "")
            activo_legible = "Activo" if u.get("activo") else "Inactivo"
            if estado["rol"] != "Todos" and rol_legible != estado["rol"]:
                continue
            if estado["activo"] != "Todos" and activo_legible != estado["activo"]:
                continue
            if (
                texto
                and texto
                not in f"{u.get('username', '')} {u.get('vinculacion', '')}".lower()
            ):
                continue
            resultado.append(u)
        return resultado

    def render():
        visibles = usuarios_filtrados()
        table_holder.content = create_table_container(
            header=header_table,
            rows=[usuario_row(u) for u in visibles]
            or [
                ft.Container(
                    padding=20,
                    alignment=ft.Alignment(0, 0),
                    content=ft.Text(
                        "No hay usuarios que coincidan con los filtros.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                )
            ],
        )
        pagination_holder.content = create_pagination_bar(
            texto_info=f"Mostrando {len(visibles)} de {len(estado['usuarios'])} cuentas registradas",
            pagina_actual=1,
            total_paginas=max(1, (len(visibles) + 9) // 10),
        )
        try:
            page.update()
        except Exception:
            pass

    async def recargar():
        try:
            estado["usuarios"] = await api_client.get_usuarios()
        except Exception as err:
            mostrar_error(f"No se pudo refrescar la lista de usuarios: {err}")
        render()

    def on_buscar(e):
        estado["texto"] = search_field.value or ""
        render()

    def on_filtrar_rol(e):
        estado["rol"] = filtro_rol_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    def on_filtrar_estado(e):
        estado["activo"] = filtro_estado_dropdown.value or "Todos"
        render()

    search_field.on_change = on_buscar
    filtro_rol_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_rol
    filtro_estado_dropdown.on_change = on_filtrar_estado

    # ==================== 3. DIÁLOGO NUEVO USUARIO ====================
    async def abrir_nuevo_usuario(e):
        try:
            alumnos = await api_client.get_alumnos(solo_activos=True)
        except Exception:
            alumnos = []
        try:
            profesores = await api_client.get_profesores(solo_activos=True)
        except Exception:
            profesores = []
        try:
            padres = await api_client.get_padres()
        except Exception:
            padres = []

        ids_rol = {r.get("nombre"): r.get("id_rol") for r in estado["roles"]}

        username_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Usuario *", hint_text="ej: jperez", width=380
        )
        password_field = ft.TextField(
            label="Contraseña *", password=True, can_reveal_password=True, width=380
        )
        rol_field = ft.Dropdown(
            label="Rol *",
            value="Alumno",
            width=380,
            options=[
                ft.dropdown.Option(r)
                for r in ["Administrador", "Docente", "Padre / Tutor", "Alumno"]
            ],
        )
        vinculo_field = ft.Dropdown(label="Vincular a", width=380, options=[])
        error_text = ft.Text("", size=12, color=BADGE_RED_TXT, visible=False)

        def opciones_vinculo(rol_legible: str) -> list[ft.dropdown.Option]:
            sin_vinculo = ft.dropdown.Option(key="", text="Sin vínculo")
            if rol_legible == "Docente":
                return [sin_vinculo] + [
                    ft.dropdown.Option(
                        key=f"profesor:{p['legajo']}",
                        text=f"{p['apellido']}, {p['nombre']} (#{p['legajo']})",
                    )
                    for p in profesores
                ]
            if rol_legible == "Padre / Tutor":
                return [sin_vinculo] + [
                    ft.dropdown.Option(
                        key=f"padre:{p['id_padre']}",
                        text=f"{p['apellido']}, {p['nombre']} (DNI {p['dni']})",
                    )
                    for p in padres
                ]
            if rol_legible == "Alumno":
                return [sin_vinculo] + [
                    ft.dropdown.Option(
                        key=f"alumno:{a['legajo']}",
                        text=f"{a['apellido']}, {a['nombre']} (#{a['legajo']})",
                    )
                    for a in alumnos
                ]
            return [sin_vinculo]

        def on_rol_change(e):
            vinculo_field.options = opciones_vinculo(rol_field.value or "")
            vinculo_field.value = ""
            vinculo_field.update()

        rol_field.on_change = on_rol_change
        vinculo_field.options = opciones_vinculo(rol_field.value or "")

        async def guardar(e):
            username = (username_field.value or "").strip()
            password = (password_field.value or "").strip()
            rol_legible = rol_field.value or ""

            if not username or not password:
                error_text.value = "Usuario y contraseña son obligatorios."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return

            id_rol = ids_rol.get(ROL_API.get(rol_legible, ""))
            if not id_rol:
                error_text.value = "No se pudo resolver el rol seleccionado."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return

            payload: dict = {
                "username": username,
                "password": password,
                "id_rol": id_rol,
                "activo": True,
            }

            vinculo = vinculo_field.value or ""
            if vinculo:
                tipo, ident = vinculo.split(":", 1)
                if tipo == "alumno":
                    payload["legajo_alumno"] = int(ident)
                elif tipo == "profesor":
                    payload["legajo_profesor"] = int(ident)
                elif tipo == "padre":
                    payload["id_padre"] = int(ident)

            try:
                await api_client.create_usuario(payload)
            except httpx.HTTPStatusError as err:
                detalle = "Error al crear el usuario."
                try:
                    detalle = err.response.json().get("detail", detalle)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                error_text.value = f"⚠️ {detalle}"
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return
            except httpx.RequestError:
                error_text.value = "⚠️ Error de conexión con el backend."
                error_text.visible = True
                error_text.update()
                return

            page.pop_dialog()
            await recargar()

        dlg = ft.AlertDialog(
            modal=True,
            title=ft.Text("Nuevo Usuario", size=18, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD),
            content=ft.Column(
                spacing=14,
                width=380,
                tight=True,
                controls=[
                    ft.Text(
                        "Cree la cuenta y vincúlela a un alumno, docente o tutor si corresponde.",
                        size=13,
                        color=MUTED,
                    ),
                    username_field,
                    password_field,
                    rol_field,
                    vinculo_field,
                    error_text,
                ],
            ),
            actions=[
                ft.TextButton("Cancelar", on_click=lambda e: page.pop_dialog()),
                ft.Button(
                    "Crear Usuario", bgcolor=TEAL, color=BG_WHITE, on_click=guardar
                ),
            ],
        )
        page.show_dialog(dlg)

    btn_nuevo_usuario.on_click = abrir_nuevo_usuario

    aviso_seguridad = ft.Container(
        bgcolor=TEAL_LIGHT,
        border_radius=BORDER_RADIUS,
        padding=14,
        content=ft.Row(
            spacing=10,
            controls=[
                ft.Icon(ft.Icons.LOCK_OUTLINE, size=18, color=TEAL),
                ft.Text(
                    "Seguridad y Privacidad: Cada rol accede únicamente a las secciones autorizadas. Los padres solo pueden consultar y gestionar información de sus propios hijos vinculados.",
                    size=13,
                    color=TEAL_DARK,
                    weight=ft.FontWeight.W_500,
                ),
            ],
        ),
    )

    render()

    # ==================== 4. RETORNO CON ADMIN_LAYOUT ====================
    return admin_layout(
        page=page,
        titulo="Control de Acceso, Usuarios y Permisos RBAC",
        active_route="/admin/usuarios",
        content_controls=[
            aviso_seguridad,
            aviso_error,
            filters_bar,
            table_holder,
            pagination_holder,
        ],
    )


======================================================================
--- RUTA: app\pages\alumno_dashboard.py ---
